## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent BMW identity, with a dark-to-light blue gradient, full-sentence action titles, strong photography and a clear section order from vision to financials, outlook and contacts. The execution falls short in the places that matter most. Key numbers and emphasised words on slides 20 and 45 are teal or blue on a blue gradient and lose contrast, and the dual margin lines on slide 45 are confusingly labelled, with unlabelled dots at the right edge and a margin line that crosses the bars. Many slides (13, 34, 35, 41, 50, 56) pack small body text, tiny map labels and footnotes onto busy backgrounds, so they read as a reference document and are weak as presented slides. Slides 7 and 10 add a roadmap triangle and brand grid, and slides 61 to 65 are plain disclaimer and data tables.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent identity, the full-sentence titles, the photography and the staged three-column logic of slide 26.

**Most useful repair:** Raise the contrast of highlighted numbers on slides 20, 45 and 48, label the two margin series directly on slide 45, and enlarge the map labels on slides 13, 35 and 50.

**What would change the score:** I would cut text density on the long capability slides (21, 30, 34, 41, 56) and keep one message per page, moving the detail to an appendix.

_Confidence high. 65 of 65 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This investor relations deck from BMW Group, dated November 2026, opens with a title slide and then sets out a vision of premium individual mobility. Its strategy rests on brands, powertrains, technology and people, and sustainability. The middle sections cover the Neue Klasse platform, the battery and Gen6 roadmap, a software-defined vehicle and AI architecture, and factory and supply chain setup, with the global footprint and charging network shown on regional maps. A sustainability block follows, covering the net zero path to 2050, renewable energy at plants, supply chain decarbonisation and the iX3 product footprint. The financial section comes last, covering sales, segment EBIT margins, Financial Services, funding, treasury, capex, R&D, free cash flow, dividends and share buybacks, followed by the 2026 outlook. The deck closes with investor relations contacts, the 2027 financial calendar, a forward-looking statements disclaimer and consumption and emission data. Slides worth studying include the Neue Klasse quantum-leap slide, the layered SDV versus AI-defined vehicle diagram on slide 26, and the segment EBIT chart on slide 45.

## Questions about this deck

**How many pages does the BMW Group investor relations deck have?**

The BMW Group investor relations deck dated November 2026 has 65 pages. Slide 2 introduces the deck's structure, with its sections on vision, strategy and financials.

**What does the BMW Group deck say about the Neue Klasse 6th generation powertrain and battery in terms of range?**

On slide 20, the deck says the Neue Klasse with its 6th generation powertrain and battery technologies brings +30% in range through +20% volumetric energy density on cell level. It also states up to 906 km electric range (WLTP).

**What CO2 fleet emission figure does the BMW Group deck report for 2025?**

Slide 16 shows CO2 emission in the EU of 90.0 g/km for 2025, down from 99.5 in 2024. The slide states that in 2025 the BMW Group stayed -2.9 gram below target.

**How is the BMW Group investor relations deck structured?**

Slide 2 lays out three sections: 1 Vision, 2 Strategy, and 3 Financials. The strategy section is built around Brands, Powertrains, Technology & People, and Sustainability.

Full-bleed split slide: a photograph fills the left half and fades into a blue gradient on the right. A white-outlined frame sits on the right at mid-height, with a four-line left-aligned statement inside it. The footer has a source line at bottom left and a page number at bottom right, both in small grey text on a white strip.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph, taken from a high angle, of a paved urban courtyard in daylight. Three BMW sedans are parked diagonally: a black one at the top centre, a deep blue one in the middle, and a pale grey-green one with a BMW kidney grille at the lower left. A woman in dark clothes stands at the lower centre holding a red helmet, looking toward the cars. A green planted area with three cylindrical concrete columns sits at the top, with a patterned dark-green façade behind it. A light strip drain line runs diagonally across the paving. Sunlight and shadows fall across the ground. The photo's right edge dissolves into a vertical blue gradient, dark navy at the top (#0F2D5E) to bright blue at the bottom (#3A80C8). A thin white-to-transparent outlined rectangle frames the text: a bright line along the top-left corner and the right and bottom edges, fading toward the other ends. No logos.

The statement is white #FFFFFF, uppercase, in a condensed sans-serif (BMW Group TN Condensed, regular), about 4.8% of the slide height. Frame lines are thin white, about 2px, with no radius or shadow. The footer is grey #595959 condensed sans, about 1.8% of the slide height. A white strip about 4% tall runs along the bottom.

**Layout**

Full-width two-line title at top left. Left column holds three left-aligned statements spaced evenly down the slide, each marked at the left edge by a short cyan line. Right side has a framed text block with cyan bracket lines, and a full-height car photo bleeding off the right edge. White footer strip at bottom with the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

A photograph of a silver-grey BMW i7 luxury sedan, front three-quarter view, driving on a curved mountain road with a yellow edge line. Blurry mountain ranges and a pale blue-to-peach sky are behind it, with soft warm evening light. The photo is a tall rectangle that bleeds off the right edge, with the dark asphalt in the lower half. Two short horizontal cyan lines with a glow, fading to the right, start at the left edge beside the first and second statements. A cyan L-shaped bracket frames the right text block, with a top line and a left vertical line at upper left and a bottom line and a right vertical line at lower right, both fading at the ends. The words 'focus on our customers', 'reconcile economy, ecology and society' and 'Our values' are underlined in white.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2A57 at the top through royal blue #2A68B8 in the middle to light blue #3E86CC and a greyish pale blue #8FA9C6 at the bottom, above a white footer strip. The title is white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Technical Condensed Regular caps, about 4.8% of slide height. Body text is white in the same condensed sans-serif regular at about 3.3%. The highlight words are bold cyan #65DBFB at about 4.8%. The footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. The cyan lines are thin and glowing, with no rounded corners.

### Slide 5

**Text**

THE NEUE KLASSE REPRESENTS NEXT LEVEL MOBILITY WHICH DRIVES
PROGRESS ON BOTH A PRODUCT AND A COMPANY LEVEL.
The new design language of the Neue Klasse combines progressive clarity with timeless
elegance, reflecting BMW's DNA.
The powertrain delivers maximum efficiency and next-level performance through
intelligent energy management and a future-oriented architecture – regardless of the
propulsion system.
Strategic partnerships for
battery technology, software &
AI capabilities create a tailored
customer experience worldworld.
From a collaborative organisation to
new working models
and clustering of development,
production & purchase expert teams.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left, flush to the left edge and cropped to a portrait rectangle: a red BMW coupe-SUV in rear three-quarter view parked on pale sandy ground beside a deep blue sea. The sky is clear blue with thin wisps of cloud, and the light is bright daylight. Dominant colours are blue, sand beige and red. Short thin tan/gold gradient line at the photo's right edge, around y 34%, fading out to the right. Two callout frames with thin tan/gold L-shaped borders, each with a top and a right/bottom edge fading out, with a slight shadow. No other graphics.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2A56 at the top to bright blue #3A7FC8 in the middle and pale grey-blue #8FA9C6 near the bottom, above a white footer strip. Key phrases are underlined in white, and the highlighted phrases 'partnerships' and 'new working models' are bold in tan #D8BDA2 at about 4.8%. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. ### Slide 6

**Text**

AS A TRUE SDV1, THE NEUE KLASSE BUNDLES BMW'S INNOVATIVE STRENGTH
IN THE CORE AREAS OF DRIVING DYNAMICS AND DIGITALISATION.
Driving dynamics
Digitalization
Scalable technology clusters can be integrated across all segments and models, enabling outstanding vehicle performance. This incorporates four high-performance computers 'The 4 Superbrains'2 and next level ADAS technology 'Smart, Safe und Symbiotic'.
Introducing a completely new dimension of intelligence with a fully digital, continuously updateable platform that enables an immersive user experience and a deeper connection between human and vehicle. Virtual and real worlds merge inside the car thanks to the new BMW Panoramic iDrive with digital features creating a driver-focused, personalised experience.
1Software Defined Vehicle I 2Heart of Joy is only available in BEVs with Gen6 drive; all vehicles have a zonal architecture with central high-performance compute.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 6 -

**Layout**

Two-line title top-left across about 88% width. Below, two equal columns (left x 4-48.5%, right x 51.7-96%), each with a photo at the top, a caption label overlaid on the photo's top-left, and a white-outlined text box beneath. Footnote right-aligned under the boxes; white footer strip at the bottom with the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Left photo: a blue BMW SUV (Neue Klasse iX3) driving left to right on a sunlit road, with motion-blurred palm trees and a hedge behind it, blue sky at upper right. The photo fades into the dark blue background at the top. Right photo: over-the-shoulder interior shot of a man and a woman in the front seats of a BMW. Both hold yellow-orange phone game controllers and play a game shown on the wide curved dashboard display. The BMW logo is on the steering wheel. The scene is dark, with warm skin tones, and the photo fades into the blue background at the top. Each photo has a thin white line along its bottom edge. A thin white outline rectangle surrounds each column, photo plus text, with a white rule between photo and text. Key phrases in the body text are underlined in white.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0B2A5B at the top to light blue #6FA0D6 at the bottom, with a white footer strip over the bottom 4%. Vertical gradient background from deep navy #0B2A5B at the top to bright blue #3A7DC9 in the middle and a pale light blue #8FB4DC near the bottom. White title in BMW Group Condensed (a narrow sans-serif), regular weight, all caps, about 4.8% of slide height. White body text in the same condensed sans at about 3.3% of slide height, with underlined highlights. Footnote is white, about 1.7%. Footer strip is white with grey #595959 text at about 1.8%. Outlines are thin white lines, about 2px, with square corners, and there are no shadows.

### Slide 7

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 7 and 10 add a roadmap triangle and brand grid, and slides 61 to 65 are plain disclaimer and data tables.

**Text**

ACCELERATING INNOVATION AND PRODUCT MOMENTUM WITH OVER 40 MODEL
LAUNCHES ACROSS THE PORTFOLIO, INCLUDING ALL POWERTRAINS BY 2027.
Launch of Neue Klasse
with BMW iX3.
The first of the new era gets all future updates
via backward integration,
continuously developing hardware &
semiconductors.
2025
2026
2027
Illustrative only
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 7 -

**Layout**

Full-width dark navy header with a two-line uppercase title at left (4% margin). A photo panel sits on the left edge, with a text callout beside it. A rising triangular timeline graphic runs across the lower right with car images, year labels beneath, and a white footer strip with source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Left: a cropped photograph (about 26.6% wide) of a grey BMW iX3 Neue Klasse SUV driving toward the camera on a sandy road at sunset, with warm golden light, dunes and a hazy sky behind, bleeding off the left edge. Callout frame: a thin beige/gold L-shaped bracket line at the top-left and bottom-right around the 'Launch of Neue Klasse' text, with a fading line. Timeline: a large right-angled triangle wedge rising from bottom-left to upper right, filled with a gradient from bright cyan (#17C0F0) at bottom-left to deeper blue at right, fading out toward the top. Two thin vertical light-grey divider lines mark the years: one at about x 37.4 (between 2025 and 2026) and one at about x 66.4 (between 2026 and 2027). Cars: 2025 section has one small blue-grey BMW iX3 (front three-quarter view). 2026 section has a beige/silver BMW 7 Series sedan, a grey-green BMW X5-type SUV, a red BMW X6-type coupe SUV and a blue BMW M5-type sedan, climbing the slope. 2027 section has about 17 dark-blue covered/shrouded car silhouettes (SUVs, sedans, coupes) scattered across the right side, growing in count toward the top-right. A white footer strip lies below the gradient.

**Design**

Background: Navy #0F2650 at top fading through blue #1F5FB0 to light blue-grey #8FB0D4 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Deep navy to mid-blue vertical gradient background with a pale blue-grey fade at the bottom above a white footer strip. Title in white uppercase condensed sans-serif (BMW Group Technical Narrow/Condensed Regular) at about 4.8% of slide height. Body text white at about 3.3%, with underlined key phrases. Accent beige #D8BDA2 used in bold for 'BMW iX3' and the '2025' label. Year labels about 2.6% in white; footer text grey #595959 at 1.8%. Thin gold bracket lines, no shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 8

**Text**

THE BMW GROUP SETS ICONIC IMPULSES BY RETHINKING PREMIUM INDIVIDUAL
MOBILITY CONTINUOUSLY AND DISTINCTIVELY FOR ALL ITS BRANDS.
1
VISION
Our path to the future.
2
STRATEGY
Our ambition to excite our customers.
Brands I Powertrains I Technology & People I Sustainability
3
FINANCIALS
Our robust results.
Strong brand designs, demand-oriented offers across all powertrains with continuously developed technology - balancing costs and sustainability.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 8 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed background photo with a two-line title at top left. Below it a three-column agenda row (1 Vision, 2 Strategy, 3 Financials) separated by two thin vertical lines, with the middle column highlighted. A white outlined frame in the lower part holds a two-line statement, and two cars sit at the bottom. Footer text sits at bottom left and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed photograph fading from deep navy blue at top to brighter royal blue in the middle, then into a dark rocky volcanic cliff and gravel road at the bottom. Two BMW cars in side profile face each other at the bottom, centred: a matte grey-bronze sedan on the left and a metallic emerald-green estate on the right. They are lit by cool dusk light with black wheels. A thin white rectangular outline frame runs from about x 4% to 96% and y 56% to 96%. The top edge fades out toward the right and the right edge fades toward the top. Two thin vertical gold-grey divider lines sit at about x 29% and x 68%. The large numerals 1 and 3 and their headings are faded to low opacity, grey-blue. The numeral 2 and STRATEGY are in gold. A translucent white box sits behind the faded 1 column text and another behind the 3 column text.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed photograph, deep navy #0F2A5A at top, blue #2461B0 in the middle, dark rocky landscape at the bottom. Dark navy to royal blue vertical gradient photo background #0F2A5A to #2461B0. Title is white #FFFFFF, all caps, in a condensed sans-serif (BMW Group TN Condensed Regular), about 4.8% of slide height. Body text is white condensed sans-serif at about 3.7%. The active section numeral and heading are bold gold #968847. Inactive sections are faded to about 30% opacity. The frame outline is thin white at about 2 px. The footer is grey #595959 at 1.8%. No shadows or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the left half and fades into a blue gradient panel on the right. A thin white outlined frame sits in the right half at mid-height, containing a left-aligned four-line statement. Footer text sits at the bottom left and the page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Aerial photograph of cars and motorbikes arranged in a circle on a pale grey-blue studio floor with soft shadows. Top left: a silver BMW sedan with a dark glass roof. Top centre-right: a yellow MINI hatchback with a black Union Jack roof. Bottom left: a red classic Mini with a white roof and a number 37 roundel. Bottom centre: a dark red classic BMW sedan. Dark vintage motorbikes appear at the left edge and right-centre. The lighting is soft and cool, and the colours are silver, yellow and red against grey-blue. The photo is cropped to the left half and fades into the blue panel through a gradient. A thin white line frame (open at the top right and bottom left, where the line fades to transparent) surrounds the text block.

**Design**

Background: Blue vertical gradient from navy #12294F to bright blue #2F78C4 on the right, with the car photograph full-bleed on the left fading into it. Deep navy-to-bright blue vertical gradient on the right (about #12294F at top to #2F78C4 at bottom), with the photo blending in from the left. White uppercase condensed sans-serif headline (BMW Group Technical Narrow Condensed style) at about 4.8% of slide height, regular weight. Footer in grey #595959, about 1.8% of slide height. Frame is a thin white line of about 2px with fading ends, with no shadow or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left across nearly full width. Below it, four equal columns outlined by thin white lines, each with a logo at top, a two to four line white description centred, then a photo panel with a centred white tagline over its top. A full-width outlined banner with financial services logos sits below, and a white footer strip with source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four brand logo groups in white: BMW roundel with M logo and Alpina logo linked by thin diagonal lines; MINI winged logo with a vertical line to John Cooper Works logo; ROLLS-ROYCE MOTOR CARS wordmark; BMW roundel with BMW MOTORRAD wordmark. Four photos: a dark blue BMW X7 SUV kicking up sand in orange desert dunes; a green classic Mini and a mint Mini parked on a street with a woman in a blue top and a child walking in front, mural behind; a green Rolls-Royce Spectre coupe on grass with trees, palm and white building behind; a person in a green jacket holding a helmet standing beside a white and blue BMW motorcycle on a coastal dune road. Thin white outlined rectangles frame each column and the banner. Financial banner has the dark navy ALPHABET wordmark and the ALPHERA Financial Services logo with blue dot pattern in dark text.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0B2A5B at top to light blue #5C9AD6 at bottom, with a white footer strip along the bottom 4%. Deep navy blue (#0B2A5B) at top fading to a lighter blue (#3A7BC8) and pale blue-grey at the bottom. White titles and body text in BMW Group Condensed regular. Title about 4.8% of slide height in capitals; body text about 3% and taglines about 3.3%. Thin white 2px outlines, no rounded corners, no shadows. Footer strip white with grey #595959 text.

- 11 -

**Layout**

Two-line title at top left. Below, a horizontal light-blue rounded arrow band with three labelled sections (Heritage, Sponsoring & partnerships, Global platforms & new technologies) runs across the middle. Overlapping square photo tiles with white captions sit above and below the band, and the BMW and MINI logos sit at the right. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Roundel logo of BMW (blue and white quadrants in a white ring) at top right, about x 92-96%, y 21-28%. MINI winged logo (white outline wings with a circular MINI badge) at right, about x 89-96%, y 63-69%. A wide light-blue (#65DBFB at 70%) rounded loop band with a darker navy inner pill, and two translucent white chevron arrows pointing right between the sections. About 20 overlapping square event photos with a darkening gradient behind captions. Top row, left to right: a BMW M1 race car near a villa (Concorso d'Eleganza); a golfer mid-swing on a course (BMW Golfsport); a Cannes festival palm logo on a dark teal car (Festival De Cannes); a BMW booth with a building at CES Las Vegas; a BMW stand with cars and a roundel (Auto China Beijing). Overlapping them: a white, red and blue BMW racing car at speed (Goodwood Festival of Speed); a blue BMW XM with tennis posters (Tennis BNP Paribas Open); pink and red art with cars on the floor (Art Basel); a dark blue tunnel with a light-trail car (Auto Shanghai); a dark BMW in front of a building with red DLD letters (DLD Munich). Bottom row: a vintage MINI with people (Goodwood Revival); a Cooper's Corner Shop with a yellow MINI and crowd (Goodwood Festival of Speed); an aerial view of colourful MINIs with MINI UNITED lettering; hot-air balloons over a field (MINI Takes the States); a man sitting on a wooden crate shaped like a car (Japan Mobility Show Tokyo); a MINI display on a building facade (Auto Shanghai); a toy-like city model with cars (Auto China Beijing); a red MINI and an Underground sign (IAA Munich).

**Design**

Background is a vertical blue gradient from deep navy at the top to lighter blue at the bottom, with a white footer strip. The title is white, uppercase, regular-weight condensed sans (BMW Group Condensed style). Captions are white condensed sans, except DLD I Munich in navy #173B68. Section labels are white condensed sans, larger. Footer text is grey #595959. The band is a rounded loop shape with translucent cyan fill and no shadows.

- 12 -

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left over a dark navy header. Subtitle below at left. A world map fills the left two-thirds with four circular callouts on regions. A tall night photo sits on the right edge from about y 21% to 92%. The 1,708k callout is at lower right, overlapping the map and the photo edge. A white footer strip has the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

A world map as a flat, semi-transparent blue-grey silhouette of the continents. Four circles in dark navy (#173B68, 80% opacity) sit over it. USA is over North America, Europe over Europe, China over East Asia, and the Asia-Pacific/Eastern Europe/Middle East & Africa circle over southern Africa and South America. Each circle holds a bold region name or figure and BEV/xEV percentages. At right is a vertical photo of a night-time race track (Yas Marina-style circuit) with a row of colourful BMW M cars parked in a line along the track. The cars are red, orange, yellow, green, cyan and a purple-maroon XM SUV in the foreground, with a red touring car at right. The track has a turquoise kerb on the left edge and floodlights, tents and grandstands in the background under a black sky. The photo is cropped and sharp-edged, running to the right edge of the slide. A teal bracket-like thin line frame surrounds the 1,708k text, open at the top right and bottom left. A short teal gradient line sits at the left edge under the subtitle at about y 29%.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #12285A at the top to mid blue #3A7BC8 and then pale grey-blue #8BAAD0 at the bottom, above a white footer strip. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed Regular, a condensed sans-serif. Title is in capitals at about 4.8% of slide height. Region names and figures are in bold white at 2.6%. The 1,708k figure is bold teal #269CA6 at 4.8%. The footer text is grey #595959 at 1.8%. Teal accent lines #269CA6 are thin. The circles are flat with no shadow.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark navy header area with a two-line all-caps title at top left and a two-line underlined-keyword subtitle below. A thin light-blue horizontal line separates the header from a world-map globe that fills the lower ~58% of the slide, with country flags and plant labels placed over the regions. A translucent callout box sits at the lower centre; a footnote line runs across the bottom of the map and a white footer strip holds the source text at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Night-view globe of the Earth, curved horizon rising across the middle, blue-lit with light-blue atmospheric glow along the horizon and dark navy space above. Cyan map-pin markers with thin white leader lines connect labels to locations: Mexico, USA, Brazil, UK, Germany/Europe, Austria, Hungary, Egypt, South Africa, India, Malaysia, Thailand, Indonesia, Vietnam, China. Country flags above the labels: Mexico, USA, Brazil, UK, Germany, Austria, Hungary, Egypt, South Africa, India, Malaysia, Thailand, Indonesia, Vietnam, China. Small white outline icons before site names: car/vehicle plant icon, motorcycle plant icon, electric-model icon. A translucent white 20% box with light-blue border holds the 'Our principle' callout. Thin light-blue gradient lines at both ends of the header/map divider.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy gradient #10284F to blue, with a night-earth globe image across the lower part and a white footer strip. Dark navy gradient background (#10284F) fading into blue globe imagery; white text for title, subtitle and labels (#FFFFFF); accent cyan #65DBFB for 'local for local.' and map pins. Typeface BMW Group Technical Narrow/Condensed sans-serif, regular weight, bold for the cyan tagline. Title about 4.8% of slide height, all caps; subtitle 3.3%; site labels 2.0%; footnotes 1.7%. Keywords in subtitle underlined. Footer strip white with grey #595959 text. Callout box has a 20% white fill and a light-blue outline, no shadow.

**Layout**

Full-bleed split slide: a motion-blurred car photo fills the left ~59% and fades into a blue gradient on the right. A four-line left-aligned white headline sits inside a thin partial white frame in the right half, vertically centred. Footer at the bottom: source line at left margin, page number at right.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left: a silver-grey BMW 7 Series style luxury sedan with a large twin-kidney grille and slim headlights, shown in three-quarter front view driving left to right on grey asphalt (plate M DI 285E). The background is a tiered, green hedge-covered building facade with strong horizontal motion blur, in greens and browns, with daylight lighting. The photo is cropped to the left side and dissolves via a soft horizontal fade into the blue background at about x 45-59%. Over the right side is a thin white outline frame (about 1.5px) with gradient-faded lines at the top-left and bottom-right: top edge from the left corner fading toward the middle, left edge short, right edge starting midway and running down to the bottom-right corner, bottom edge fading in from the middle. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Vertical blue gradient from navy #0F2D5E at the top to #3A80C8 at the bottom, with the car photograph on the left fading into it. Blue vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2D5E at the top to mid blue #3A80C8 at the bottom right. Headline in white #FFFFFF, uppercase, light-weight condensed sans-serif (BMW Group Technical Condensed Regular) at about 4.8% of slide height. Footer text in grey #595959, condensed sans-serif, about 1.8% of slide height. Frame lines are white, thin, with no shadows or rounded corners.

**Text**

THE BEST DRIVE TECHNOLOGY FOR EVERY NEED.

HIGHLY EFFICIENT ICE
DIESEL / PETROL
PLUG-IN HYBRID
BEV
NMC / LFP
HYDROGEN

**Layout**

Two-line left-aligned title at top-left with a 4% margin, followed by a two-line intro paragraph indented slightly. A large outlined frame holds four columns of centered headings, each with a car chassis image below. A centered note sits near the bottom of the frame. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four cutaway 3D renderings of BMW car chassis in three-quarter top view, each fading softly into the blue background with a white glow on the left three. 1) Highly efficient ICE: silver and grey chassis with a front combustion engine, driveshaft, exhaust and wheels. 2) Plug-in hybrid: similar chassis with an engine, an orange high-voltage cable and a battery along the floor. 3) BEV: flat silver battery floor with orange cabling, an electric drive unit at the rear and wheels. 4) Hydrogen: darker, blue-toned chassis with a fuel cell stack and tanks, vignetted into the background. A white thin-line rectangular frame outlines the section. It runs from x 4% to 96%, y 39% to 92%, and its top and bottom edges fade out toward the middle. There are no logos.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2A5C at the top to bright blue #3A82CC and light blue-grey #8AAAC8 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Vertical gradient background, deep navy #0F2A5C at the top shading to bright blue #3A82CC near the bottom and pale blue-grey #8AAAC8 above the footer. Title, body and headings are white #FFFFFF. The typeface is BMW Group Technical Narrow, a condensed sans-serif. The title is uppercase regular at about 4.8% of slide height, the intro text is about 3.3%, column headings are bold uppercase at about 3%, subheadings are regular uppercase at about 2.2%, and the note is about 2.6%. The footer strip is white with grey #595959 text at about 1.8%. The frame uses a white line about 3px thick with a soft shadow and a gradient fade. There are no rounded corners.

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left on a dark navy band. Bar chart occupies the left two-thirds, with a callout box at centre right and a tall photo on the right edge. White footer strip with source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart of CO2 emission in EU in g/km, 5 vertical bars, y-axis 0 to 140 in steps of 20 with no gridlines, thin light baseline. Bars are outlined in white with a transparent fill showing the blue gradient. Values: 2021 115.9, 2022 105.0, 2023 102.1, 2024 99.5, 2025 90.0. The 2025 bar is filled with a gradient from muted blue-grey at the top to dark navy at the bottom. Dashed light-blue target lines sit above each bar: 2021 about 126, 2022 about 125, 2023 about 124, 2024 about 122, 2025 about 93. Legend at bottom left: a dashed line with 'CO2 target BMW Group'. A short light-blue horizontal accent line sits at the left edge under the chart subtitle. The callout has a thin light-blue bracket outline (top and left lines plus a bottom and right corner line). The photo at the right edge shows a red BMW and a blue BMW driving down a steep curved concrete ramp or banking, against a blue sky with white clouds. It is shot from above with motion blur, in grey, white and blue tones, and is cropped to a tall rectangle bleeding to the right edge.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #11285A at the top through blue #2A66B0 to pale blue #8FAAC8 at the bottom, with a white footer strip at the bottom 4%. Vertical gradient background from dark navy at the top to bright mid-blue and then pale light blue at the bottom. A white footer strip runs along the bottom. White text is set in BMW Group Condensed (a condensed sans-serif). The title is in uppercase, regular weight, about 4.8% of slide height. The highlight '-2.9 gram' is bold in muted steel blue #7BA1BE. Footer text is grey #595959. Flat, no shadows, square corners.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the right edge, cropped and boxed, flush to the right edge: an orange MINI Aceman SUV seen from the rear three-quarter on a sunlit city street corner. It sits at a crosswalk beside a traffic light pole showing green, with a yellow building and glass office towers behind, a street sign reading 'Grand St' and a speed limit 35 sign. Warm daylight, blurred background, dominant colours orange, yellow, blue-grey. Two callout boxes have olive-green L-shaped corner brackets (top and left edge at top-left, right and bottom edge at bottom-right) that fade out at the ends. A short olive-green horizontal accent line fades out at the left edge at about 62% height. Some key phrases are underlined in white: 'By 2030', '>50% of global sales', 'high efficiency and low consumption', 'overall footprint'. No charts or logos.

- 18 -

**Layout**

Dark blue header with a two-line all-caps title at top left and a two-line intro sentence below it. Beneath a thin light-blue line, a world map fills the lower area with location labels in three columns (left, centre, right) and a legend at bottom left. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

A semi-transparent grey-blue world map on a blue gradient. A circular inset (dark navy ring, light outline) in the centre magnifies a light-grey silhouette of Germany. Small teardrop map pins in light blue (Gen5 series), beige (Gen6 series) and gold (Gen6 pilot) sit on Germany, the US east coast, Mexico, Hungary and north-east China. Thin white leader lines link pins to labels. Rounded rectangle labels with soft shadows each carry a circular flag icon: USA (Spartanburg, Woodruff), Mexico (San Luis Potosí), Hungary (Debrecen), Germany (Leipzig/Regensburg/Dingolfing, Irlbach-Straßkirchen, Parsdorf/Munich/Hallbergmoos) and China (two Shenyang). Label colours: light blue = Gen5 series (Spartanburg, Leipzig I Regensburg I Dingolfing, Shenyang top); beige = Gen6 series (Woodruff, San Luis Potosí, Debrecen, Irlbach-Straßkirchen, Shenyang bottom); gold = Gen6 pilot (Parsdorf I Munich I Hallbergmoos). The legend at bottom left has three coloured swatches: gold, beige, light blue. Thin glowing light-blue horizontal line fades in from the left and right edges at about y 34%.

**Design**

Background: Navy-to-blue vertical gradient (#102A56 at top to #1B6FC0 and lighter blue #8FB0D6 near the bottom) with a white footer strip. Dark navy #102A56 header fading into a mid-blue #1B6FC0 gradient and light blue near the bottom of the map area. White title and body text in BMW Group Condensed regular, sans-serif. The title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height; the intro is about 3.3%; labels are about 2.2%. The intro has the words 'close coordination between pilot and series production plants' underlined. Accents: light blue #00A1DA, beige #D8BDA2, gold #D2A000, each at 80% opacity. Labels have small rounded corners and subtle drop shadows. The footer is grey #595959 text on a white strip.

### Slide 19

**Text**

THE BMW GROUP CONTINUES TO EXPAND ITS BATTERY EXPERTISE ALONG THE
ENTIRE VALUE CHAIN, UNDERSCORING ITS COMMITMENT TO CIRCULARITY.
Development
Pilot production
Purchasing
Assembly
Vehicle integration
We are pursuing a consistent zero-defect approach and integrate highly intelligent,
AI-supported quality checks into the production process.
Recycling of high-
voltage batteries
Battery Cell Competence
Center (BCCC) - Munich
Cell Manufacturing
Competence Center (CMCC) –
Parsdorf
Cell Recycling Competence
Center (CRCC) – Salching
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 19 -

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left across the full width. A row of five equal photo tiles beneath, each with a label at its top-left. A light-blue loop arrow runs behind and below them, with a centered statement inside the loop and a recycling box at right. Three larger photo tiles with captions sit in a row at the bottom, and a white footer strip carries the source at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Eight photographs with thin white borders and blue-tinted overlays. Top row, left to right: a gloved hand holding a small cylindrical cell in lab equipment; a technician at a pilot production machine with a screen; a smiling woman in a white protective suit holding a cell; two workers in blue assembling a battery pack on a yellow fixture; an overhead view of workers around a vehicle chassis with a white battery pack. Bottom row: a technician in a mask and blue gloves working at a bench; an aerial view of a large factory building with green fields; recycled cell samples on a white table, a cylinder with a recycling symbol, a rolled foil and black powder in a dish. A semi-transparent cyan (#65DBFB, 70% opacity) loop arrow starts at the left with an arrowhead pointing right at the top row, curves down around the left, runs along the bottom and curves up at the right. A white recycling icon (two circular arrows) sits above the white-outlined rectangle at right containing 'Recycling of high-voltage batteries'.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from dark navy #12295A at the top to bright blue #3C7FC8 near the bottom, with a white footer strip along the bottom. Dark navy-to-mid-blue vertical gradient background. White title and labels in BMW Group Condensed regular sans-serif. 'zero-defect' is bold cyan #65DBFB. Title is about 4.8% of the slide height; labels and body about 3.3%. Photos have thin white outlines, and the recycling box has a 1-2px white outline with no fill. The bottom footer strip is white with grey #595959 text.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Three large percentage callouts with small footnoted qualifiers sit beside a volume ramp chart, making the Gen6 gains easy to scan at a glance.
- Review: Key numbers and emphasised words on slides 20 and 45 are teal or blue on a blue gradient and lose contrast, and the dual margin lines on slide 45 are confusingly labelled, with unlabelled dots at the right edge and a margin line that crosses the bars.
- Suggested repair: Raise the contrast of highlighted numbers on slides 20, 45 and 48, label the two margin series directly on slide 45, and enlarge the map labels on slides 13, 35 and 50.

**Text**

**Layout**

Dark navy title band across the top with a two-line left-aligned title. Below, a car photo fills the left ~27% of the slide; to its right a white-outlined bracket frame holds five key-figure callouts in two rows (3 on top, 2 below) separated by vertical white dividers. Under it a chart heading, a stacked area chart and legend. Footnote strip at the bottom of the blue area, then a white footer with source text left and page number right.

**Visuals**

Photo: a blue BMW sedan (Neue Klasse i3) driving on a curving coastal mountain road, shot from a high front three-quarter angle, with rocky cliffs on the left and the sea and sky behind; slight motion blur on the road; cropped box at left, bleeding off the left edge. White thin line frame (open at top left, thicker at right and bottom) around the key figures, with a fade at the top and left edges. White vertical divider lines between callouts: one between 'in range' and 'Up to 906 km', one between 'Up to 906 km' and '+20%', one between '-40 - 50%' and '+30%'. Stacked area chart 'Volume development BMW 2022 – 2028': x-axis years 2022-2028, y-axis 0% to 100% in 25% steps. Series bottom to top: ICE (grey-beige #A7A08B, about 83% in 2022, about 77% in 2024-2026, about 62% in 2028), PHEV (light tan #D8BDA2, thin band about 8-10%), BEV Gen5 (dark blue #194890, about 7% in 2022 rising to about 15% in 2024-2025, then narrowing to about 5% by 2028), BEV Gen6 (white, appearing from 2025-2026 and widening to about 30% in 2028). A white arrow labelled 'Ramp-up' runs horizontally above the chart from about 2025 to 2028. A white double-headed vertical arrow at right of 2028 marks the range, with labels '~90% Gen6' in white and '~10% Gen5' in dark blue. Legend below chart with square swatches: white BEV Gen6, dark blue BEV Gen5, tan PHEV, grey-beige ICE. Footnote markers superscript.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy at the top to mid blue (#2F74C4) and lighter blue toward the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title white, uppercase, BMW Group Condensed regular sans-serif, about 4.8% of slide height. Key figures in bold condensed sans in mid blue #2967B1 at 4.8%, descriptive text white regular at 3.3%. Chart labels white at 2.0%. Footnote white at 1.7%. Footer text grey #595959 at 1.8%. No shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 21

**Text**

EACH GENERATION OF OUR BATTERY SYSTEM DEVELOPMENT IS SHAPED BY
TECHNICAL ADVANCES, SPECIFICATIONS AND REGULATORY MEASURES.
Integration
High integration to optimise battery system energy per volume (e.g. cell-to-pack, pack-to-chassis).
Fully flexible cell production technology (pouch, cylindrical, prismatic).
Energy
Higher capacity materials and optimised cell filling factor.
Safety
Safety on battery system level has highest priority.
Sustainability
Persistently increasing share of recycled and reused materials are fed back into new production cycles.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 21 -

**Layout**

Title in two lines across the top left. Below it a row of four equal-width columns, each with a photo on top, a heading overlaid at its top-left, and a white-outlined text box beneath the photo. White footer strip with source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs, each boxed in a column with a thin white border. 1) Integration: factory workers in dark blue polo shirts assembling a battery pack with orange cables and yellow/orange transport carts; bright industrial hall. 2) Energy: close-up of cell production machinery with large beige/copper rollers, metal clamps and blue hoses; grey/brown tones. 3) Safety: two men in navy shirts standing beside a silver-grey BMW sedan charging at a plug, green trees and a building behind. 4) Sustainability: a smiling bearded man in white sleeves and blue vest with teal gloves lifting a dark battery foil sheet over a yellow barrel filled with crinkled copper foil; industrial hall in the background. Photos fade into the dark blue at their tops. Text boxes have thin white outlines. Some key phrases are underlined in white.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #10285A at top to mid blue #2F78C4 to pale blue near the bottom, ending above a white footer strip. White title and body text in BMW Group condensed sans-serif, regular weight. Title in capitals, about 4.8% of slide height. Column headings and body about 3.3%. Footer text grey #595959, about 1.8%. White 2px outlines on the photos and text boxes, square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 22

**Text**

OUR WORLDWIDE CHARGING INFRASTRUCTURE STRATEGY LEVERAGES MULTIPLE
SOLUTIONS TO DELIVER REACH & PERFORMANCE IN THE CHARGING NETWORK.
IONNA
North America
7 major global automakers I 1 JV I
>30,000 high-powered charging
points I urban & highway
IONITY
Europe
24 countries I >840 locations I
>5,900 charging points I
city centre & highway
IONCHI
China
JV with MB I >460 locations I
Public high-powered charging I
>2,500 charging points
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 22 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark blue slide with a two-line all-caps title across the top left. Three staggered columns (North America left and lowest, Europe centre, China right) each have an outlined bracket box with logo, region label and three lines of text, and a circular photo below. A white footer strip holds the source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Background is a dark navy-to-mid-blue gradient with a stylised globe at the bottom: a lighter blue horizon glow and dark navy continent silhouettes (North America, Europe, Africa, Asia). Three circular photos with thin light-blue outlines. Left: a night-time charging canopy with a red-lit 'POWERED BY IONNA' sign and a dark BMW SUV charging beneath it. Centre: a grey BMW sedan charging beside a white and magenta charger at daytime, with mountains, wind turbines and a cloudy sky behind. Right: a Chinese charging station under a grey canopy with a Mercedes and BMW logo and Chinese characters, a teal BMW SUV and a white car, with two people standing, at dusk with pink-blue light. Each text group is framed by a thin light-blue bracket outline, open at the top-right and bottom-left corners (top and right edges plus left and bottom edges). Logos: IONNA (white wordmark with orange dot on the i), IONITY (white wordmark with a bird-like swoosh), IONCHI (white wordmark with a dark and grey swoosh mark).

**Design**

Background: Dark navy to mid-blue gradient (#0F2A5C to #2A6AB5) with a globe illustration at the bottom; white footer strip (#FFFFFF). Dark blue gradient background (#0F2A5C at the top to #2A6AB5 near the globe), white title and body text (#FFFFFF), light-blue accent for region labels (#609ACC) and bracket outlines. Condensed sans-serif (BMW Group Condensed) regular for the title and body, bold for region labels. Title is about 4.8% of slide height in capitals. Body is about 3.3%. Footer is grey (#595959) text on a white strip, about 1.8%. Circular image crops with thin blue strokes, no shadows.

### Slide 23

**Text**

HYDROGEN FUEL CELL TECHNOLOGY PROVIDES AN ADDITIONAL PILLAR IN THE
BMW GROUP'S FUTURE DRIVE SYSTEM PORTFOLIO.
The new BMW X5 will be the brand's first vehicle that offers customers a choice
between five different powertrain options –
Battery electric, plug-in hybrid, gasoline, diesel, and now also hydrogen fuel cell.
Our 3rd generation fuel cell technology and systems will be manufactured in Steyr.
Prototypes are already under development at our Munich and Steyr competence centres,
and key components are supplied by our Landshut technology hub.
BMW Group and Toyota Motor Corporation take collaboration to the next level to offer Fuel
Cell Electric Vehicle (FCEV) options for passenger cars.
TOYOTA
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 23 -

Full-width title at top left over two lines. Three left-aligned text blocks stacked in the left two thirds, separated by two short horizontal gold lines at the left edge. A white footer strip along the bottom holds the source line at left and the page number at right.

Photograph on the right, cropped to a tall rectangle and bleeding off the right edge: a white BMW X5 hydrogen prototype with camouflage-style lettering and the plate 'M TU 415E', seen from the front three-quarter angle on a dark stone stepped platform. Moody blue-grey cloudy sky with a rocky cliff at the right edge. Cool, dim lighting with a dark foreground. Two short gold-to-transparent gradient lines sit at the left edge, at about 40% and 62% of the slide height. The BMW roundel logo (blue and white) is next to a thin white vertical divider and the white TOYOTA emblem and wordmark. Underlines on 'choice between five different powertrain options', 'collaboration' and 'next level'.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #10285A at the top to bright blue #3A7FC8 around 80% down, fading to pale #8FA9C4 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Vertical gradient background from deep navy at the top to bright medium blue in the lower middle, fading to pale grey-blue near the footer. The footer is a white strip. Title and body text are white in BMW Group Condensed (a light-weight condensed sans-serif). The title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height; body is about 3.3%. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Accent lines are gold-beige #C9A882 fading to the right. Some phrases are underlined in white. No shadows and no rounded corners.

### Slide 25

**Text**

BMW'S SOFTWARE-DEFINED VEHICLE APPROACH DELIVERS ON OUR DIGITAL
CUSTOMER PROMISE.
Five key pillars are key to realizing the SDV potential.
Scalability
of hardware & software layers leverages development across segments.
Updateability
keeps features & platforms fresh over generations.
Evolutionary code-base across generations ensures feature continuity & software re-use.
'Platformisation'
decouples function development from fast-moving enablers.
Globalisation
of developer network effectively uses independent contributions.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.

Title in two lines at top left. A photo is boxed at the right edge, overlapping a teal bracket frame. White footer strip with source text at left and page number at right.

Dark, nearly black photo/render at the right edge showing the silhouette of a car with a glowing light-blue highlight line along the roofline. Glowing teal digital particles and circuit-like traces stream along the lower body. Two glowing teal turbine-style wheels sit at the lower left of the image, with a reflection beneath. A thin teal L-shaped bracket line runs along the top and left of the content area and along the bottom, ending at the photo. Three short vertical white divider lines between the pillars in the top row and one in the bottom row. Footer is a white strip.

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #11284F at top through blue #2F78C6 to pale blue-grey #8FA8C4 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Deep navy-to-bright-blue vertical gradient background, fading to a pale grey-blue near the bottom above a white footer strip. ### Slide 26

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A three-column progression diagram shows how the architecture moves from software-defined to AI-defined, using consistent cloud and data labels across each stage.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent identity, the full-sentence titles, the photography and the staged three-column logic of slide 26.

**Text**

BUILDING ON OUR SDV COMPETENCE WITH NEUE KLASSE, WE WILL TRANSITION
TO THE NEXT LEVEL WITH AI-DEFINED VEHICLE SOLUTIONS.
Software-defined Vehicle
AI-powered Vehicle
AI-defined Vehicle
CLOUD & BACKEND
Data, updates & services
CLOUD & DATA
Training & models
CLOUD & DATA
Continuous learning, digital twin
Software defines functions
& enables updates.
AI in the vehicle extends
functions & assists.
AI as central orchestrator is
the architecture driving value,
safety & experience.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 26 -

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left. Three equal columns across the slide, each with a centered heading above a photo, and below it a two-tier text panel with a white outline. Chevron arrows sit between the columns. White footer strip at the bottom with source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Three photos side by side. Left: BMW interior with steering wheel, hand on a curved touchscreen, grey-white dashboard, dark cabin. Middle: close-up of a driver's hand on the steering wheel next to a tilted infotainment screen showing a 3D map with green trees and a blue route. Right: dark near-black image with a faint car silhouette outline and glowing teal circuit-like lines and a wheel graphic. Below each photo is a white-outlined panel split by a thin light-blue horizontal line: left panel tinted dark grey-blue, middle panel tinted light grey-blue, right panel tinted mid blue. Two pale white-blue chevron arrows point right, between left and middle and between middle and right panels. No logos or charts.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2755 at top to bright blue #3C80CC around 75% and pale blue-grey #8FB0D0 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. All slide text is white in BMW Group Condensed (narrow sans-serif). Title is regular uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height. Column headings are about 3.3%, panel headings bold uppercase at 2.6%, panel body regular at 2.6%. Panels have a 2px white outline, semi-transparent fills and square corners. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%.

### Slide 27

**Text**

A COMPLETELY NEWLY DEVELOPED DIGITAL NERVOUS SYSTEM IS BEING
LAUNCHED ACROSS ALL DRIVETRAINS, MAKING A LEAP AS A SDV1.
Four high-performance computers, also called ‘The 4 Superbrains’, consolidate the
computing power for the key customer functions of:

Brain of BMW Panoramic
iDrive

- 27 -

**Layout**

Full-width title at top left over a dark navy header. Below, a car image panel on the left (about 27% wide) with callout labels, and on the right an intro sentence above a 4-column by 3-row grid with row labels in a left column. Footnote line sits above a white footer strip with the presenter line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Left: a rectangular, uncropped-edge-to-edge render of a dark blue, semi-transparent X-ray style SUV seen from the front three-quarter, with glowing cyan wiring and small blue/cyan computing modules in the front area, on a light grey background. Four white thin leader lines with right angle bends run from labels (Heart of Joy, Brain of BMW Panoramic iDrive, Brain of automated driving, Core brain) to the modules. A short white gradient line sits at the top right of the image near the intro text. Right: table with white-outlined cells (thin white 2px borders) on the gradient. Header row has 4 transparent cells with white text. Below are white 70% opacity rounded-less rectangles: a full-width bar for Software applications, four cells for SW platforms, a full-width bar for Middleware, four cells for Hardware. Row separator white lines. Underlined text in the intro sentence: ‘The 4 Superbrains’ and computing power for the key customer functions.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #10285A at top to mid blue #2565B5 and lighter blue #6A9FD0 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title in white uppercase BMW Group Condensed (condensed sans-serif), regular weight, about 4.8% of slide height. Body text white about 3.3% for the intro, 2.6% for labels. Cell text dark navy #133155. Footnote white 1.7%. Footer grey #595959 at 1.8%. Cells are translucent white rectangles with square corners, no shadows.

### Slide 28

**Text**

A ZONAL ARCHITECTURE FEATURING INTELLIGENT, DIGITISED ENERGY
MANAGEMENT CREATES A SCALABLE BASIS FOR THE SDV.
Future-proof basis for the growing number and complexity of digital functions in the SDV,
including growing AI capabilities, which create intelligent experiences for our customers.
The associated stability on the hardware side enables a further decoupling of hardware
and software and is the basis for a fast-cycle and future-proof development of the SDV.
-30%
weight reduction.
-600m
of cabling.
Increased diagnostics
capability & security.
Automated
production.
Significant cost reduction
through zonal architecture &
automation.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.

Left photo, cropped and flush with the left edge: a translucent ghost-like BMW car seen from the front three-quarter view. Its wiring harness is shown in bright multicolour (red, green, blue, magenta, purple, cyan), and the body is pale grey-white on a light grey-blue background with soft studio lighting. A short horizontal cyan line with a fade to the right sits at the photo's right edge, about 32% down. A cyan-outlined rectangular frame with a glowing gradient edge, open at the top right and bottom left, encloses the figures. Two white vertical divider lines: one at about x=50 running from the top of the frame to the bottom, and one at about x=66 running through the top row only.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #10264F at the top to steel blue #8FA8C4 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Vertical gradient background from deep navy #10264F at top through royal blue #2A6FC0 to pale steel blue #8FA8C4 at the bottom, ending in a white footer strip. ### Slide 29

**Text**

WE MARK A MILESTONE IN REAL DIALOGUE WITH OUR VEHICLES, COMBINED WITH
INTELLIGENT CAPABILITIES.
The driver can ask complex questions related to both
the vehicle and general knowledge topics and get into a
natural dialogue with a free choice of words.
First car manufacturer to
add the Amazon Alexa+ AI
technology to its vehicles.
When linked to a personal Amazon account, the BMW Intelligent Personal Assistant
currently provides access to personalised music streaming as well as sports and news
content through integrated third-party services. Additional functionalities, such as
message retrieval and food ordering, are planned for future availability.
amazon alexa
The technology will soon be launched with the iX3 in Germany and will subsequently
follow in other derivatives and markets.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 29 -

**Layout**

Two-line all-caps title across the top at left margin. Left column holds three text paragraphs stacked down the slide, separated by thin gold rules at the left edge. A callout with a gold bracket sits in the upper middle, and a tall car-display screenshot is on the right edge. A white footer strip has the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Right side: a full-height-ish screenshot of the BMW iDrive display with the Alexa assistant. The top shows a road with an orange-pink sunset glow and city buildings in perspective. Below is a dark bar with a blue orb avatar, '+2 min' and a small album icon. Below that is a dark map with 3D blocks, a 'Suggestions' row with Weather, Parking Payments and Drive Recorder icons, and a Spotify player showing 'Beautiful Things' by Benson Boone with playback controls. A gold L-shaped bracket line frames the 'First car manufacturer' callout, with a thin top line and a bottom line. Two short gold gradient horizontal lines sit at the left edge at about y 40% and y 67%. A BMW roundel logo, a thin white vertical divider and the 'amazon alexa' wordmark with a ring icon sit at the end of the second paragraph. Key phrases are underlined in white: complex questions, natural dialogue, personalised music streaming, sports and news content, integrated third-party services, in Germany, other derivatives and markets.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2653 at the top to bright blue #3A82CC and pale blue #8FB0D0 near the bottom, above a white footer strip. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed regular, with the title at about 4.8% of slide height and body at about 3.3%. 'First' is bold in beige-gold #D8BDA2. Accents are thin gold lines #C9AE8C. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. No shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 30

**Text**

THE BMW PANORAMIC iDRIVE REVOLUTIONISES OUR VEHICLE OPERATION AND IS
A RE-INTERPRETATION OF OUR 'HANDS ON THE WHEEL, EYES ON THE ROAD'.
BMW Panoramic Vision
3D Head-Up Display
Multifunction
steering wheel
Central display
Projecting personalised, key driving information visibly across the windshield, creating an immersive and spatial experience for both driver and passengers.
Seamlessly integrating advanced navigation and driving assistance in one view above the Panoramic Vision, showcasing BMW's cutting-edge innovation with perfectly synchronised projection technologies.
Featuring BMW's Shy-Tech concept with illuminated, tactile buttons that provide intuitive, haptic feedback, enabling effortless control without taking your eyes off the road.
Introducing Matrix Backlight technology and an enhanced QuickSelect menu structure for intuitive touch control, and with an ergonomic free-cut design positioned close to the steering wheel.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 30 -

**Layout**

Two-line title across the top at left margin 4%. Below it, four equal-width columns side by side, each with a photo on top carrying an overlaid heading and a description box beneath with a thin white outline. White footer strip at the bottom with source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Four photographs across the row, each about 22% wide and 34% high, with a white line under each. 1) BMW Panoramic Vision: a car interior with a slim, wide display strip running across a light cream dashboard, a white steering wheel rim at lower right, soft bright light, cream, white and dark grey. 2) 3D Head-Up Display: a view through the windshield down a dark tunnel with road markings and red and green lights, a small projected display graphic above a dark instrument strip reading 48, dark and blurred, black, grey, hints of red and green. 3) Multifunction steering wheel: a white and grey steering wheel with the BMW roundel and buttons on the spokes, a wide curved display behind it, a bluish textured dashboard, light grey and white with blue ambient accents. 4) Central display: a hand with the index finger touching a large curved touchscreen with a blue map of a coastline and menu icons, dark interior with metallic trim, blue and dark grey. The top of each photo fades into the navy background through a gradient. Each description box below has a thin white outline and a semi-transparent blue fill. A narrow lighter-blue vertical strip runs along the right edge. The footer is a white band.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy at the top to bright medium blue in the middle, fading to pale blue at the bottom, above a white footer band. Title, headings and body text are white in BMW Group Condensed regular, a condensed sans-serif. The title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height, headings and body about 3.3%. Some key phrases in the body text are underlined in white. Box outlines are white at about 2px with square corners and no shadows. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%.

### Slide 31

**Text**

FOR EUROPE, BMW IS THE FIRST OEM WITH APPROVAL FOR ADAS SYSTEMS
ACCORDING TO THE NEW UN ECE REGULATION (DCAS).
Customer-centric UI powers safety-in-use: Driver engagement, responsiveness,
controllability, reproducibility.
UNECE
Highway assistant with hands-off function – UN R171 DCAS homologation:
Lane change with eye confirmation and lane-recognition redundancy.
Strict prevention of misuse by robust driver monitoring.
Plausibility checks within system.
Strict compliance with speed limits (limited vMax with ADAS, no permanent offset).
Highest safety standards enable
hands-off driving
in line with regulation.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.

The steering wheel is white/light grey with a BMW roundel, with a curved display behind it. Daylight shows a blurred motorway and hillside through the windows. Light grey/white seats and cool blue ambient light strips. UNECE logo on the right at about 86.5–95.6% x, 29–34% y: white UN emblem (globe in olive wreath) with bold white spaced capitals 'UNECE'. ### Slide 32

**Text**

WE INVEST IN SMART, STRATEGIC PARTNERSHIPS FOR THE NEXT GENERATION
ADAS, CREATING A CUSTOMISED CUSTOMER EXPERIENCE.
For global markets (ex China)
For China
Jointly developed for the Neue Klasse, the ADAS platform
emphasises safety, maturity, and high performance. It
combines hardware, software, and AI expertise to
leverage cost synergies and the latest AI technologies,
featuring a flexible SoC design and advanced computer
vision capabilities, driven by a strong joint commitment
to quality and excellence.
Building on over 20 years of safety expertise, the
Navigation Guided Assist leverages advanced AI and
local driving knowledge to deliver seamless L2+
assistance tailored for China's roads, debuting in the
locally-produced BMW iX3 in 2026 and expanding
across future models.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 32 -

**Layout**

Full-width title top-left over two equal side-by-side cards (left and right) with about 4% outer margins. Each card has a photo on top with a caption label overlaid at its top-left, and a text block below with partner logos at bottom right. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Left photo: a grey-silver BMW Neue Klasse SUV (licence plate M NK 1517 E) driving on a sand dune, low sun flare at upper left, warm golden light, dark ridge with grass at top, shot at a three-quarter front angle, boxed with a thin white border. Right photo: a group of about 35 people in white T-shirts standing and giving thumbs-up around a camouflage-wrapped prototype BMW SUV on a paved plaza, daylight, boxed with a thin white border. A dark gradient overlay sits behind each caption at the top of the photos. Thin white outline frames surround each card, with a white line under each photo. Bottom of the left card: BMW roundel, thin white vertical divider, Qualcomm wordmark above a red Snapdragon icon with the word Snapdragon. Bottom of the right card: BMW roundel, thin white vertical divider, white Momenta logo (stylised A mark with the word momenta). Key phrases in the body text are underlined in white.

**Text**

OVER-THE-AIR (OTA) SOFTWARE UPDATES ENABLE US TO FLEXIBLY ENHANCE

Rolls-Royce.

is industry benchmark.
>160 upgrades
since 2019 for features &
quality.

Infotainment, driving,
ADAS, body & comfort.

BEV, ICE, PHEV.

Infotainment
'Curve-Ahead View',
video streaming,
in-car gaming,
parking payments.
ADAS
Remote control parking,
trailer assistant,
front collision warning
with brake intervention.
Body
Exterior light
animations.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 33 -

**Layout**

Full-width white title in two lines at top left. A one-line subtitle sits below it. The left two-thirds holds three text columns (key figures, 'All' statements, recent examples) separated by thin vertical lines inside a teal outlined frame. A vertical strip of five image panels runs down the right edge. A white footer band at the bottom has the source text at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Right-edge strip of five vertical panels, about 120px wide each, running from y about 20% to 92%. Panel 1: a photo of a blue BMW front end parked on a yellow-marked tarmac under blue sky. Panel 2: a dark navy panel with a microphone icon and 'IPA' text at top, a white BMW globe logo with 'BMW' in the middle, and a music-note with sound-waves icon at bottom. Panel 3: a solid red panel with a close-up of a black BMW steering wheel with the roundel. Panel 4: a photo of a grey BMW from above on a road with blue and red light effects, and green foliage at the top. Panel 5: a teal panel with a hand holding an orange phone that shows a BMW app screen with 'ALL GOO' text, cropped at the right edge. A thin teal rectangular outline frames the left content area, running from x about 4% to 76%, y about 32% to 88%. Thin white vertical divider lines separate the columns, and thin teal vertical dividers separate the three example sub-columns.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2B5B at the top to bright blue #3C7FC8, fading to pale blue #8FB0D4 at the bottom, with a white footer band. The background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2B5B at the top to bright blue #3C7FC8 in the middle, fading to pale blue #8FB0D4 near the bottom, above a white footer band. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF. Accent numbers and keywords are bold teal #269CA6. The typeface is BMW Group TN Condensed, a condensed sans-serif. Title is in all caps at about 4.8% of slide height. Subtitle and body text are about 3.3%, example text about 2.6%, and the footer about 1.8% in grey #595959. The sub-headings Infotainment, ADAS and Body are underlined. Lines are thin with no shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 34

**Text**

BMW iFACTORY INTEGRATES FLEXIBLE PRODUCTION, SUSTAINABLE INNOVATION, / ADVANCED DIGITALISATION, AND PEOPLE AT THE CORE OF TRANSFORMATION.
Virtual factory planning & / manufacturing
Artificial Intelligence / Quality Next
Catena-X open data / ecosystem
Physical AI & / humanoid robots
Integrating AI-driven / planning and 3D human / simulations to enable early / collaboration and / seamless simulation of / complete production and / assembly processes.
AIQX sets a new standard / in quality testing with / advanced visual and / acoustic inspection / capabilities for mobile and / static applications.

**Layout**

Full-width two-line uppercase title at top left. Below it, four equal-width columns, each with a photo on top carrying a white overlaid heading and a white-outlined text box beneath with body text. A white footer strip at the bottom holds the source line on the left and the page number on the right.

**Visuals**

Four photographic panels at the top of the columns. 1) Screenshot of a 3D virtual factory planning tool: a colourful factory floor with yellow, orange and red conveyor and rack models, tiny human figures, car body shells in the background, a toolbar and a thumbnail library strip of equipment icons along the bottom. 2) Photo of a worker in a blue BMW Group jacket holding a smartphone showing an inspection app, next to an open grey-blue car door, in a bright workshop. 3) Dark blue world map with a network of white connecting lines and about six small yellow-and-white circular Catena-X node icons, mainly over North America, Europe and Asia. 4) Photo of a white humanoid robot on a wheeled base facing a man in a dark blue work uniform and blue gloves, in a industrial hall with metal machinery and shelving. Each text box has a thin white outline, and thin white rules sit at the bottom of each photo. A thin lighter-blue vertical strip runs down the right edge.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2A5C at the top to mid blue #2A6BBF and lighter blue #6FA3D8 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Deep navy-to-mid-blue vertical gradient background fading to lighter blue near the bottom, with a white footer strip. White title and body text in BMW Group condensed sans-serif (BMWGroupTNCondensed Regular). Title about 4.8% of slide height in uppercase; column headings and body about 3.3%. Key phrases are underlined in white. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Text boxes have a 2px white outline, no shadows and square corners.

**Layout**

Full-bleed world-map background with a two-line left-aligned title at top. A highlight box sits at left under the title. Five semi-transparent grey hub cards are arranged around the map: two in the upper middle/right and three in a lower row. A legend row and a footnote sit near the bottom, above a white footer strip with the footer text at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark-blue world map with a network of connected nodes and lines, and concentric-circle location markers. Country name labels (ROMANIA, PORTUGAL, USA, CHINA, INDIA, SOUTH AFRICA) are in semi-transparent light blue. Cyan dots mark BMW Tech Offices: USA at left, and two in China. Dark teal dots mark R&D Centres: Munich/Romania area, and two near China. Five round flag icons sit at the top-left of the cards: Romania, China, Portugal, South Africa, India. Company logos sit in the cards: BMW Group TechWorks Romania, BMW IT TechWorks China with a vertical white divider line and BA TechWorks logo, Critical TechWorks swirl logo, ZA Hub logo, BMW BTI TechWorks India logo. The left highlight box is a translucent white fill with a cyan outline. The legend uses a grey square, a cyan circle and a teal circle as markers. A white strip runs along the bottom.

**Design**

Background: Dark navy-to-bright-blue gradient (#0B2A5B to #3A7BC8) with a full-bleed network world map; a white footer strip at the bottom. Dark navy to bright blue gradient background (#0B2A5B at top to #3A7BC8 at bottom) with a map overlay. Title in white (#FFFFFF), uppercase, BMW Group Condensed-style regular sans-serif, about 4.8% of slide height. Body text is white condensed sans-serif at 2.6% in the cards, and the legend is at 2.2%. The highlighted '>10,000' is bold cyan (#65DBFB) at 4.8%. Cards are grey #BFBFBF at 70% opacity with square corners and no shadow. Separator bars 'I' are bold. Footer text is grey (#595959) at 1.8%.

### Slide 36

**Text**

EDUCATION IS THE ENGINE FOR CHANGE AT THE BMW GROUP, DRIVING GROWTH,
AND SOCIAL STABILITY THROUGH INNOVATIVE AND RESPONSIBLE ACTION.
With approximately 155,000 employees from 110 nations, we live lifelong learning as it is
fundamental for our success, enables technological innovation and its transition.
We encourage personal responsibility and growth and offer exciting training opportunities
in an inspiring and stimulating environment.
The BMW Group Academy
provides >1 million
training options a year –
worldwide.
~17.7h training per employee per year.
Invested € 377.5 million in 2025 in education.
We are convinced that a wide range of perspectives and talents strengthen our company
and we are proud of a 22% share of global female top management.
1Figures EOY 2025.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 36 -

**Layout**

Full-width title at top-left over two lines. Photo boxed at left edge, below the title, about a quarter of the width. Right of the photo is a column of text blocks starting at x≈29.5%: two paragraphs, then a framed key-figures panel split by a vertical divider into two parts, then a closing paragraph. Footnote at the right above a white footer strip with the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Photograph at left, cropped at the left edge: a young man with dark hair, white t-shirt and a BMW lanyard, looking down and working on a silver HP laptop. Behind him is the open boot of a white car with server or test equipment racks and black cabling. Yellow coiled network cables lie at the lower right of the photo. A rainbow heart sticker is on the laptop. Lighting is bright and cool indoors, with white, black and yellow dominant. Also a thin gold-beige gradient horizontal line at about y 34% left of the text, and a gold-beige L-shaped bracket frame: a top line from the photo edge running right and fading, a left vertical line, and a right vertical and bottom line around the key-figures panel. A thin white vertical divider sits between the two key-figure columns. Several parts of the gold frame are faded gradient lines. Underlined phrases: lifelong learning, fundamental for our success, opportunities in an inspiring and stimulating environment, 22% share of global female top management.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2754 at the top to light blue #7FA5C8 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Vertical gradient background from dark navy #0F2754 at the top through mid blue #2A6BB8 to light blue-grey #7FA5C8 near the bottom, over a white footer strip. White titles and body text in BMW Group Condensed (a narrow humanist sans-serif), regular weight. The title is in capitals at about 4.8% of slide height. Body text is about 3.3%. Key figures are in bold gold-beige #D8BDA2 at about 4.8%. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Thin gold-beige #C9B093 frame lines, no shadows and no rounded corners.

### Slide 37

**Text**

OUR LONG-TERM ECONOMIC
SUCCESS GOES HAND-IN-HAND
WITH ENVIRONMENTALLY &
SOCIALLY RESPONSIBLE ACTION.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 37 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed split slide: a photograph occupies the left ~50% and fades into a blue gradient on the right. A four-line left-aligned uppercase statement sits in the right half, vertically centred, inside a thin white corner-bracket frame. Footer text at bottom left and page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Full-height photograph on the left: close-up of a woman's single eye with a green-blue iris, dark eyebrow and long lashes, peeking through a large split green tropical leaf (alocasia-like). The leaf fills the frame in teal and mint greens with pale veins. Soft natural lighting, warm peach skin tone in the gap. A faint overlay of geometric lines, arcs and grid patterns (technical blueprint style) is across the photo. The photo's right edge fades smoothly into the blue background. A thin white frame outlines the text: a top-left bracket (top line and left side) and a bottom-right bracket (right side and bottom line), with the lines fading toward their free ends.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #12285A at the top to mid blue #2E6BB8 and lighter sky blue #4A86CC at the bottom. Main statement is white #FFFFFF, uppercase, in the BMW Group Condensed regular sans-serif (narrow), about 5.2% of slide height, tight line spacing. Footer text is small grey #595959 at about 1.8% of slide height. Frame lines are thin 2px white, with gradient fade at the ends. No shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 38

**Text**

THE BMW GROUP IS FULLY COMMITTED TO THE PARIS CLIMATE AGREEMENT AND
ACHIEVING NET ZERO BY 2050 AT THE LATEST.
We are reducing CO2 across the entire lifecycle of our vehicles – from the supply chain to production and throughout their operation to recycling.
Mid-term climate target
2035: 60 mn t of CO2e savings compared to 2019.
Main measures of our comprehensive decarbonisation strategy: increasing the share of renewable energy and secondary raw materials, electrifying our vehicle fleet and implementing product and process innovations.
Milestones on the road to net zero:
2019
2030
2035
2050
Base year:
150 mn t CO2
At least
-40 mn t CO2
Another
-20 mn t CO2
Net Zero
15 mn t CO2
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 38 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient slide with a white footer strip. Title is top-left across two lines. Left column holds three text blocks stacked with gold accent lines at the left edge, a bracketed callout sits at the upper centre-right, and a photo collage is on the right edge. A horizontal timeline runs along the bottom with four year ticks. Footer has the source left and page number right.

**Visuals**

Right-side photo collage (about 26% wide) on a pale beige-grey background. Tilted, curved photo tiles form an arc: a white building and truck, green malachite mineral, blue plastic granules, a blue perforated part, a car-body metal part at the top right, wind turbines with a car on a field, a close-up of a textured grey surface, a field of blue solar panels, and a gloved hand in a blue suit holding a cylindrical part. A gold-beige bracket frame with a thin gradient line surrounds the 'Mid-term climate target' callout, left and bottom edges, with the bottom line running into the collage. Two short gold-beige horizontal gradient lines sit on the left edge, at about y 39% and y 62%. A timeline arrow in tan (#D8BDA2) runs horizontally from x about 10% to 60% with an arrowhead on the right. Four thin white vertical ticks sit at 2019 (x about 10%), 2030 (x about 30%), 2035 (x about 38%) and 2050 (x about 55%). Several phrases are underlined in white.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2858 at the top to mid blue #2F70BF in the middle and light blue #7FA7CF at the bottom, ending above a white footer strip. White text throughout in BMW Group Technical Narrow Condensed, a condensed sans-serif. The title is uppercase regular at about 4.4% of slide height. Body is about 3.3%. Key phrases are underlined. The '2035' is bold in tan #D8BDA2 at about 4.8%. Timeline labels are about 3%, with bold values. The footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. No shadows; thin gold lines.
