### Slide 39

**Text**

OPTIMISING OUR PRODUCTION SITES' FOOTPRINT BY HARNESSING INNOVATIVE
TECHNOLOGIES & LOCAL POTENTIAL FOR ENERGY FROM FOSSIL-FREE SOURCES.
Solar: San Luis Potosí (Mexico), Dingolfing and Regensburg (Germany) integrate on-site
PV¹ systems for partial electricity supply. Debrecen (Hungary) operates as BMW Group's
first fully electrified, 100% fossil-free energy plant with ¼ generated by PV systems.
Biomass: Steyr (Austria) and Dingolfing (Germany)
utilise district heating and locally-produced heating
from biomass, while Rosslyn (South Africa) combines
on-site PV systems with biomass electricity.
Leipzig (Germany) partially powers production with
wind and hydrogen energy, and Shenyang (China)
uses geothermal energy for its powertrain plant.
Since 2020, 100 % of the
external electricity used for
production at all of the
BMW Group's plants
worldwide has come from
renewable sources.
¹Photovoltaic.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 39 -

**Layout**

Full-width title in two lines at top left. Left column holds three text blocks stacked vertically with thin gold dividers at the left edge between them. Right side has a tall aerial photo at the far right edge and a callout text box with olive bracket lines to its left and below. Footer on a white strip with source text left and page number right; footnote sits just above the strip at right.

**Visuals**

Aerial photograph of a large BMW production plant: flat-roofed factory halls with solar panels and rooftop units, green fields and trees at the top, parking lots with cars at lower right, a road and brown farmland at the bottom. Daylight, bright, grey roofs with green and tan surroundings. Placed at the right edge, cropped as a portrait rectangle bleeding off the right edge. Two short horizontal gold gradient lines fade out to the right from the left edge, at about 40% and 62% of slide height. The callout has an olive thin L-shaped bracket: top-left corner line and a bottom-right corner line with fading ends. Underlines under 'Solar', 'Biomass', 'wind and hydrogen' and 'geothermal energy'. No charts or logos.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #10264F at the top to bright blue #3A80CC around 80% height, fading to a lighter grey-blue at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Titles and body text are white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed (a condensed sans-serif) regular weight; the title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height, body about 3.3%. Highlighted words '100 %' and 'renewable sources.' are bold in olive green #808D49 at about 4.8%. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Thin olive and gold lines, no shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 40

**Text**

WE ARE PROMOTING DECARBONISATION IN OUR SUPPLY CHAIN BY FURTHER
INCREASING THE SHARE OF SECONDARY MATERIAL AND RENEWABLE ENERGY.
Upholding environmental and social standards, including human rights and ethical principles, is a fundamental priority in BMW Group's purchasing and supplier network, enforced through the 'Supplier Code of Conduct' and rigorous compliance monitoring.
We enhance supply chain transparency and traceability using risk-based mapping and innovative solutions like the Catena-X data ecosystem to ensure responsible sourcing and due diligence across our complex global supply chains.

- 40 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient slide with an all-caps two-line title across the top left. Below, a photo is bleeding off the left edge in the left quarter, with two white paragraphs to its right. Under the paragraphs, a bar chart (left) and a donut chart (right) are separated by a thin white vertical line. A white footer strip carries the source text at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Left: cropped photograph of spools of 3D-printing filament, one orange and one blue, resting on a bed of bright blue plastic granules, with a dark grey textured surface behind. Lighting is soft, and the picture is boxed and bleeds off the left edge. A short white horizontal line with a gradient fade to the right sits at the bottom right of the photo, over its edge. Bar chart: three vertical bars on a thin white baseline, labelled 2019, 2030 without measures, 2030 with measures. Bars are filled with a gradient from blue-purple at the top to orange at the bottom. The 2019 bar is short (about 40% of the 2030 without-measures height). The 2030 without-measures bar is tallest. The 2030 with-measures bar has a lower blue-orange part, with a green translucent block on top labelled Measures reaching the height of the tallest bar. A thin horizontal line marks the 2019 level across to the middle bar. A second thin line marks the with-measures level across from the middle bar. A curved arrow, dark navy to red, rises from the 2019 bar top toward the 2030 without-measures bar, with the label Electrification. A curved arrow, navy to green, descends from the top of the 2030 without-measures bar to the with-measures level. Donut chart: ring segments clockwise from the top, with centre label CO2e in orange: HV Battery 39% (teal-grey, #5A8FA0), Aluminum 26% (bright cyan #00B0F0), Steel 19% (light grey-blue #AFC5D1), Polymers 8% (paler grey-blue), Other plastics 4% (very light grey), Electronics 2% (near white), Other materials... (dark teal slice, about 2%). Thin grey leader lines connect labels to segments. A small white car icon sits beside the HV Battery label. White vertical divider line between the charts. Underlined phrases in the paragraphs: human rights and ethical principles, fundamental priority, supply chain transparency and traceability, ensure responsible sourcing.

**Design**

Background vertical gradient from deep navy #12264F at the top to mid-blue #2F6FBF in the middle and light blue #7FB0DC near the bottom, then a white footer strip. White text throughout. Title and body in a BMW Group Condensed (narrow sans-serif) regular weight, title about 4.8% of slide height in capitals, body about 3.3%, chart labels about 2.2% with bold on the without/with measures labels. Accent colours are orange #F79646 and cyan #00B0F0, with green #6B9E78. The footer is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Thin lines are white; no shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 41

**Text**

THE BMW iX3¹ SETS NEW STANDARDS IN PRODUCT SUSTAINABILITY
WITH A FOCUS ON DECARBONISATION AND RESOURCE CONSERVATION.
Production & supply chain
Parts & components
Circularity
Use phase &
lifecycle
Operating plant Debrecen without fossil fuels². 0.1t CO₂e per BMW iX3, means ⅔ CO₂e reduction compared to established BMW plants. Reducing CO₂e emissions in the supply chain by 35%.
Reduction by 42% CO₂e per Wh for battery cells³. Aluminium wheel carriers (80% SRQ) & Aluminium rims (70% SRQ). Engine cover and storage section (30% SRQ) from recycled maritime plastics.
Secondary raw material content of ~33%⁴. Econeer upholstery with strategic mono-material concept, base material 100% SRQ for the yarn using recycled PET, and disassembly optimised design.
Reaching an early break-even point with a CO₂e advantage achievable within the 1st year. Up to 20% lower energy consumption (WLTP combined)³.
¹Model: BMW iX3 50 xDrive. I ²Under normal operating conditions. I ³Compared to predecessor model. I ⁴Based on the overall vehicle.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 41 -

**Layout**

Title in two lines at top left. Below, four equal columns side by side, each with a photo on top carrying an overlaid heading and a semi-transparent blue text panel beneath with a thin white outline. Footnote line right-aligned under the panels; white footer strip at the bottom with left text and right page number.

**Visuals**

Four photographs, each boxed in a column with a white border line. 1) Production & supply chain: a woman in a blue work jacket and safety glasses handling a large pressed metal car body panel in a factory, warm orange-red glow at the top, lit from above, cropped close. 2) Parts & components: top-down view of a dark charcoal car engine bay/cover with a round BMW roundel logo in the centre, dark navy tones. 3) Circularity: close-up of grey woven upholstery fabric with blue stitching and a seam, grey-blue tones. 4) Use phase & lifecycle: a dark blue BMW iX3 parked in front of a modern wooden house with warm orange lit interior at dusk, a person standing at a wall charger with a charging cable, trees at the upper right, dark blue sky. A dark gradient overlay at the top of each photo supports the white headings. Text panels are mid-blue with thin white borders. Several key phrases in the panels are underlined in white.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2A5C at the top to brighter blue #2F6FC0 mid-slide, fading to light blue-grey #8FB0D4 near the bottom, above a white footer strip. All text on the gradient is white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed (a condensed humanist sans-serif), regular weight. Title in all caps at about 4.8% of slide height, column headings and body at about 3.3%, footnote about 1.7%. Footer text grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Superscripts and subscripts are used for footnote numbers and CO₂e. Panels have a thin white 1px outline and no rounded corners or shadows.

### Slide 42

**Text**

SOCIAL SUSTAINABILITY IS BASED ON THREE PILLARS - RESPONSIBILITY FOR
OUR EMPLOYEES, OUR PARTNERS AND FOR SOCIETY AS A WHOLE.
People: We promote leadership excellence, job stability, and continuous
employee development to create an inclusive and outstanding workplace that
values diverse perspectives and talents.
Partners: We uphold environmental and social standards across our supplier
network through transparency, traceability, and active participation in initiatives
that establish international sourcing standards.
Society: As a responsible corporate citizen, we make
a substantial contribution to society and act with
credibility toward our external stakeholders, thereby
fostering approval for our business activities.
Actively shaping a
responsible transformation
lies at the heart of the
Just Transition
approach of the BMW Group.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 42 -

**Layout**

Full-width title at top left in two lines. Left column holds three stacked text paragraphs (People, Partners, Society) with short gold rules at the left edge between them. A tall portrait photo sits at the right edge, with a call-out text box overlapping its lower left, framed by a gold bracket. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Right-side portrait photograph (cropped, flush to right edge): a smiling woman with braided hair in a navy jacket and yellow-green hi-vis vest, sitting sideways on a forklift seat in a warehouse with tall orange racking, blue and purple lit ceiling. She looks at the camera, warm lighting, dominant colours orange, navy and yellow. A thin gold L-shaped bracket frame with a fading top line surrounds the call-out text, overlapping the photo's lower left. Two short gold gradient horizontal lines on the left edge at about y 40% and y 62%. Key words are underlined in white (People, inclusive and outstanding workplace, Partners, transparency, traceability, and active participation, Society, contribution to society, act with credibility).

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #10285A at top to mid blue #3A7BC8 and pale blue-grey #9DB8D4 at the bottom, above a white footer strip. Dark navy at the top fading to a lighter blue at the bottom, ending in a pale blue-grey band above a white footer. Title in white #FFFFFF, uppercase condensed sans-serif (BMW Group Condensed Regular), about 4.8% of slide height. Body text white, BMW Group Condensed Regular, about 3.3% of slide height, with underlined emphasis phrases. 'Just Transition' in bold gold-beige #D8BDA2 at 4.8%. Gold-beige accent lines #D8BDA2 with gradient fade. Footer text grey #595959 at 1.8%. No shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 43

**Text**

THE BMW GROUP OFFERS COMPELLING FINANCIALS, A STRONG BALANCE SHEET, / INDUSTRY-LEADING CREDIT RATINGS & ATTRACTIVE STAKEHOLDER RETURNS.
1 VISION
Our path to the future.
2 STRATEGY
Our ambition to excite our customers.
Brands I Powertrains I Technology & People I Sustainability
3 FINANCIALS
Our robust results.
Sustainable profitability through inspiring products and services, a solid cash flow to fuel future investments, and consistently reliable dividend payout complemented by enhanced share buyback activities.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 43 -

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient photo background. Title in two lines at top-left spanning nearly full width. Below, three agenda columns separated by two thin vertical lines; the first two are faded, the third (Financials) is highlighted. A white-outlined frame with a summary statement sits across the lower part, over the photo of the BMW tower. Footer at bottom with left text and right page number.

**Visuals**

Background photograph of the BMW Four-Cylinder tower and BMW Museum bowl in Munich, with a white factory building roof with solar panels at lower left, and autumn-yellow tree foliage at lower right, under a pale blue sky fading from deep navy at the top. Large numerals 1, 2, 3 beside the column headings; 1 and 2 are semi-transparent grey-blue, 3 is gold. Two thin vertical gold-grey divider lines between columns at about x=29% and x=68%. A thin white rectangular outline frame (top and left edges bright, fading to right, with bottom and right edges faint) encloses the summary text, from about x=4% to x=96%, y=56% to y=96%.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #10285A at the top through royal blue #2057A8 in the middle to pale sky #DCE8F5 at the bottom, over a photo. Title is white #FFFFFF in all-caps BMW Group Condensed Regular, about 4.8% of slide height. Inactive agenda items (1 Vision, 2 Strategy) are faded grey-blue #4A6A96 at about 30% opacity. Active item 3 has a gold #968847 bold heading and numeral with white subtitle. Summary text is white, condensed sans-serif, about 3.7% of height. Footer is grey #595959, small. Lines are thin with no shadows or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Full-width title at top left over two lines. The left ~72% holds two stacked bar charts (total deliveries on top, BEV deliveries below) separated by a white horizontal line. Two framed text callouts sit in the middle-right column, and a tall car photo is on the far right. White footer strip with source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Top chart: bar chart of BMW Group total vehicle deliveries in k units, six bars: 2021 2,522; 2022 2,400; 2023 2,554; 2024 2,451; 2025 2,464 (all outlined white, transparent fill); YTD 09-2026 1,708 (filled with blue-to-navy gradient). Bars are about the same height, with a thin grey-white baseline and the value above each bar. Bottom chart: bar chart of BMW/MINI BEV deliveries in k units: 2021 104, 2022 216, 2023 376, 2024 427, 2025 442 (white-outlined bars with translucent blue fill), YTD 09-2026 315 (gradient fill from light grey-blue at top to dark navy). Growth labels in bold with small slanted grey-blue arrows between bars: +107.9% (2021 to 2022), +74.1% (2022 to 2023), +13.5% (2023 to 2024), +3.6% (2024 to 2025). Two callout boxes with thin grey-blue bracket lines at top-left and bottom-right corners and faded line accents. Short faded horizontal accent lines at the left edge beside each chart title. Photo at right: a dark blue BMW luxury sedan (7 Series) seen from the front three-quarter, parked on dark asphalt in front of a huge curved ribbed light-grey architectural wall under a blue sky with wispy white clouds; cropped tall, running to the right edge, with soft daylight.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy at the top to bright mid-blue in the middle and light grey-blue towards the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title is white, uppercase, in a BMW Group Condensed sans-serif regular, about 4.8% of slide height. Body text and chart labels are white in the same condensed sans-serif; highlighted words (increased, 2025, 17.9%, share) are bold in muted steel blue #7FA6C4. Footer text is grey #595959. Thin white/grey-blue lines, no rounded corners, soft shadows on bars.

### Slide 45

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The segment EBIT bar chart pairs each year's value with its margin in a second row, so the financial story reads cleanly without a separate table.
- Review: Key numbers and emphasised words on slides 20 and 45 are teal or blue on a blue gradient and lose contrast, and the dual margin lines on slide 45 are confusingly labelled, with unlabelled dots at the right edge and a margin line that crosses the bars.
- Suggested repair: Raise the contrast of highlighted numbers on slides 20, 45 and 48, label the two margin series directly on slide 45, and enlarge the map labels on slides 13, 35 and 50.

**Text**

**Layout**

Dark blue header band with two-line left-aligned all-caps title at the top. Below, a car photo fills the left quarter (x 0–26.6%, y 20.6–92%). A chart subtitle with a short white rule sits at top centre-right. Two teal-bracketed callouts sit in the middle-left, and a bar and line chart fills the right half. A footnote runs along the bottom of the gradient area, and a white footer strip holds the left-aligned source line and the right-aligned page number.

**Visuals**

Photo: a silver BMW SUV (Neue Klasse style, plate 'M EV333E') seen from the front three-quarter angle, driving on a road with white edge stripes at the bottom. Dusk sky fading from blue to warm grey-orange at the horizon, dark hills at left. The photo is a boxed rectangle bleeding off the left edge, with a soft blend into the background. Two teal corner-bracket frames with a fading gradient line (top and left edges plus bottom and right edges) surround the callouts: 'Outlook 2026 EBIT margin' and 'Strategic target EBIT margin'. A short white horizontal rule with a fade to the right sits under the subtitle. Combined chart with outlined, semi-transparent blue bars (white outline, no fill) for EBIT in m€: 2021 9,870; 2022 10,635; 2023 12,981; 2024 7,893; 2025 6,259; YTD 09-2026 2,875 (small bar, separate). Baseline white line under the bars. Two line series with grey-white dot markers: a solid white line for EBIT margin: 10.3% (2021), 8.6% (2022), 9.8% (2023), 6.3% (2024), 5.3% (2025); and a dashed teal line for margin excluding BBA consolidation effects: 10.3% (2021, shared start), 11.2% (2022), 10.8% (2023), 7.4% (2024), 6.4% (2025). To the right, two dots with labels 4.7%² and 3.5% for YTD 09-2026. No gridlines, no axis, no legend.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2A56 at top through blue #3A7CC8 to pale blue #7AA6D0, with a white strip at the bottom. Vertical gradient background from deep navy #0F2A56 at top to bright blue #3A7CC8 in the middle, fading to pale blue-grey #7AA6D0 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title in white #FFFFFF, BMW Group Condensed regular all caps, about 4.8% of slide height. Body and chart labels white, BMW Group Condensed regular, 2.2–3.3%. Highlight numbers '-0.5 to 0.5%' and '8 to 10%' in bold teal #25B1A7 at 4.8%. Footer text grey #595959 at 1.8%. Teal accent lines #25B1A7 about 3px, sharp corners, no shadows.

**Layout**

Title across the top in two lines, left-aligned at a 4% margin. Below, a row of five equal-width cards (photo on top, large figure and description beneath) separated by a thin horizontal gold-beige line at about 62%. Bottom band holds an Insurance card at left and a wide Serviced Retail Contracts panel with pie chart and a photo at right. White footer strip with the source text at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Five card photos with a translucent dark-blue gradient fade at top, each with a white outline box around the card: (1) Dealer Finance: aerial view of a MINI/BMW dealership with a white-roofed building, green landscaped roundabouts and car park. (2) Banking: person in a light blue button-up shirt writing with a pen on papers at a desk, face cropped out. (3) BMW Financial Services: woman with glasses in a blue blazer showing documents across a round wooden table to a curly-haired client seen from behind. (4) Alphabet: black and white BMW/MINI vehicles parked outside a building with two people talking beside them. (5) Alphera: two older people on a sofa looking at a tablet. Bottom-right photo: man in a dark jacket pointing at a laptop screen with a pen while a calculator and papers sit on a desk. Pie chart with five slices and leader lines: 18% EU Bank (light blue #609ACC), 38% Europe (teal green #1C8982), 24% Americas (mid blue #2660A9), 11% APAC w/o China, Middle East, Africa (dark navy), 9% China (beige tan). Thin white horizontal divider lines under the card headers of Insurance and Serviced Retail Contracts. No logos.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #10285A at top through blue #2660A9 to pale blue #8FB0D4 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Deep navy #10285A at top shading to brighter blue #2A66B0 and then pale blue-grey #8FB0D4 near the bottom, above a white footer strip. White text throughout (titles, headings, figures, descriptions); footer text grey #595959. BMW Group Condensed typeface (condensed sans-serif); title all caps regular, large figures regular, percentages bold. Title about 4.8% of slide height, card headings 3.3%, big figures 4.8%, descriptions 2%. Cards are rectangles with thin white 2px outlines and a semi-transparent blue fill under the figures, no rounded corners, no shadows. Gold/beige divider line fades at the ends.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue slide with a two-line title at top left. The left two-thirds holds two stacked bar charts (credit loss ratio above, EBT and RoE below) separated by a horizontal rule. A callout column sits in the middle right with two bracketed text boxes. A tall photo occupies the right edge from about 21% to 92% of the height. A white footer strip carries the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Top chart: bar chart of credit loss ratio in %, 6 outlined bars with transparent fill and thin white outlines, on a baseline. Values: 2021 0.18%, 2022 0.13%, 2023 0.18%, 2024 0.26%, 2025 0.28%, YTD 09-2026 0.35%. The YTD bar is filled with a gradient from light blue at the top to dark navy at the bottom. Bottom chart: bars of EBT Financial Services in m€, semi-transparent light-blue filled with white outlines. Values: 2021 3,753, 2022 3,205, 2023 2,962, 2024 2,538, 2025 2,401, YTD 09-2026 1,647 (a short bar). A thin grey-blue declining line above the bars shows RoE: 22.6%, 17.9%, 17.2%, 15.1%, 14.3% for 2021-2025, with no YTD point. A white horizontal rule with fade to the right at about 56% of the height. Thin pale-blue accent lines with fade at the left edge under each chart heading. Two callout brackets (thin pale-blue lines at the top-left and bottom-right corners) frame the CLR text and the RoE text. Photograph on the right, cropped rectangle: a smiling woman with dark hair in a pale pink blazer and white top holds a smartphone and looks at it, next to a smiling man with a blond bun in a tan jacket and white T-shirt. They stand at the open boot of a dark car under a concrete bridge on a sunny day with a bright blue sky. Warm natural light, dominant colours blue, pink and beige. No overlay.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #112A55 at the top through mid blue #2A6BB8 to lighter pale blue #8FB0D0 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed (a condensed sans-serif) in regular weight and uppercase for the title. The title is about 4.8% of the slide height. Section headings are about 3.3%. Chart labels are about 2.2%. Emphasis words ('solid', '13-16%', '>14%') are bold in muted steel blue #7BA1BE at about 4.8%. Footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Bar outlines are thin white lines with soft shadows. Callout brackets are thin pale-blue lines with fade. Corners are square.

- 49 -

**Layout**

Full-width title in two lines at top left over a dark navy band. Below, a photo strip on the left edge (about 27% wide), a pie chart panel in the centre with heading and callout, and a bar-style funding instruments chart in the right third separated by a thin vertical line. White footer strip at the bottom with left-aligned source line and right-aligned page number; footnotes sit just above the footer on the right.

**Visuals**

Left: full-height photo of four MINI cars (blue, red, green, yellow) parked in a row in front of a terrace of colourful townhouses (grey/blue, red, green, yellow, white) under a clear blue sky; low-angle, dark tarmac foreground, cropped box from x 0 to 27%. Centre: pie chart 'Financial liabilities as of 12/25 [in € bn]' with centre label € 110.5 bn on a pale box. Segments clockwise from top: Bonds 51.7 (teal #00716F, about 47%), ABS 21.8 (dark navy #173B68), Liabilities from Customer Deposits 18.0 (light teal #66C2C1), Liabilities to banks 7.2 (grey #70777E), Commercial paper 3.5 (beige #E3E0D5), Other 1.9 (white), Lease Liabilities & Derivative Instruments 6.4 (dark slate #32555F). White segment outlines, thin white leader lines from labels to segments, soft shadow. Right: funding instruments chart with a vertical white arrow axis pointing down labelled years: 10+, 10, 7, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1, <1, Years. Bars (no values): Brokered Certificate of Deposits (muted blue-grey, from about 5 to 1 years) over Fed Funds (blue-grey, <1); (EMTN) PPs (bright cyan, about 1 to 3 years) over CP1 (cyan, <1); ABS3 (cyan, about 2 to 3 years) with small blue-grey block beneath, over ABCP2 (cyan) with thin grey base; Banks loans (cyan, about 1 to 2.5 years); Bonds (cyan #17C3F2, tall from about 2 to 10+ years) over Customers deposits (blue-grey, <1). Legend: cyan-outlined box 'Industry-typical instruments' and grey-outlined box 'Bank-typical instruments'. Decorative white gradient-fading horizontal lines under both chart headings. A dark navy bracket-like outline frames the 'Continuing to target a varied debt structure.' callout at bottom left of the centre panel.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2A56 at top through blue #2A6FC4 to pale blue-grey #7FA3C4 at the bottom, with white footer strip. Dark navy (#0F2A56) top fading into a blue (#2A6FC4) and then pale blue-grey (#7FA3C4) gradient towards the bottom; white footer strip. White text throughout; title in white uppercase condensed sans (BMW Group Technical Condensed Regular) about 4.8% of slide height; headings about 3.3%; chart labels about 2.2%; bar labels about 1.5%; footnotes about 1.7%. The word 'varied' is bold navy #173B68 at 4.8%. Footer text grey #595959 at 1.8%. Thin white lines, no rounded corners, subtle shadows on pie.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue world-map graphic with a two-line left-aligned uppercase title at top left (4% margin). Callout labels sit in columns at the left (x 4-16%) and right (x 86-96%) edges, with others placed over the map near their locations. A white footer strip at the bottom has the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

Semi-transparent light grey-blue world map (continents only, no borders) over the gradient. White and black dots mark locations, with thin leader lines running to labels. Dots: northern Canada, western US (two, white and black, Salt Lake City), east coast US (black), Europe (two black, Den Haag and Munich), China (white and black), east China coast and Japan/Korea (white), Singapore (black), Australia (white), southern Africa (white). A teal ring circles central Europe and joins a vertical teal line to a teal-outlined rectangle at top centre holding the EMTN program text, with a teal underline below the rectangle. White leader lines go with white labels, and black lines go with grey-blue location labels. Label text sits over thin underlines.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2751 at the top to bright blue #2A6BBE in the middle, fading to pale grey-blue #8FA8C8 at the bottom of the map area. Title is white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed Regular, uppercase, about 4.8% of the slide height. Labels are about 1.8% of the slide height. White labels #FFFFFF, location labels #84A8C3 with city names in bold, teal #66C2C1 for the EMTN box text, outline and ring. Footer is grey #595959 on a white strip. Thin 1px lines, no shadows. The map has slight soft shading.

**Layout**

Full-width title in two lines at top left on a dark navy band. Below, a tall car photo is flush to the left edge. Callout text boxes with tan corner brackets sit in the middle column, and the bar-and-line chart fills the right half. A white footer strip carries the source text on the left and the page number on the right.

**Visuals**

Photo on the left edge: a dark purple-blue BMW M2 coupe seen from the rear three-quarter, driving on a bridge with blurred motion at dusk. Pastel pink, orange and blue sky, bridge arch and railing blurred in the background. Cropped box, about 27% wide, from 20.6% to 92% of the slide height. Chart: combined bar and line, with no y-axis and no gridlines. White-outlined blue bars show EBT in m€: 2021 16,060; 2022 15,860 plus a blue-and-white diagonal-hatched segment above it up to 23,509 (footnote 1); 2023 17,096; 2024 10,971; 2025 10,236; YTD 09-2026 6,128 (bar with a gradient from light blue at the top to dark navy at the bottom). A tan line with light blue dot markers shows EBT margin: 14.4%, 16.5%, 11.0%, 7.7%, 7.7%, and an isolated dot at 6.6% for YTD 09-2026. A thin white baseline runs under the bars, with two segments: 2021-2025 and YTD. No legend. Two callouts have thin tan open corner brackets (a left vertical line plus top and bottom lines with fading ends, and a right vertical line). A white short gradient rule sits under the chart subtitle at left. Small white footer band at the bottom.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy #10264F at the top to mid blue #2A66B5 in the middle and pale sky blue #7FAAD2 near the bottom, with a white footer strip. The title is white #FFFFFF, uppercase BMW Group Condensed regular, about 4.8% of slide height. The chart subtitle and callouts are white, about 3.3%. Emphasis words (significant, decline, >10%) are in bold tan #D8BDA2 at about 4.8%. Chart labels are white, about 2.2%. The footer is grey #595959, about 1.8%. Tan lines #D8BDA2 are used for brackets and the margin line. No shadows apart from a subtle one on the bars.

**Layout**

Full-bleed blue gradient slide with a two-line left-aligned uppercase title at top left. Below it a chart subtitle and a bar chart with line overlay occupy the left two thirds. A photo is boxed at the right edge with a callout text over its left side. A footnote runs right-aligned above a white footer strip with the source text at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Combo chart on the left: six bars with white outlines and a blue gradient fill, with a connected line of dots for the capex ratio. Bar values in m€: 2021 5,012; 2022 7,791; 2023 8,836; 2024 9,056; 2025 7,237; YTD 09-2026 3,657. The YTD bar is separated by a gap and has a vertical gradient from light grey-blue at top to dark navy at bottom. Capex ratio points sit above the bars: 4.5%, 5.5%, 5.7%, 6.4%, 5.4%, with a dark navy line joining the first five. The YTD 3.9% is a lone light dot with no line. Each point is a small light grey-blue dot. White baseline under the bars, with a separate short baseline under the YTD bar. No y axis and no legend. Category labels sit below the baseline. At right is a boxed photograph, cropped, of a blue BMW electric car with its bonnet open on a factory assembly line, with a steel-grey ceiling structure, yellow safety railings, and a bright metallic floor, in cool daylight. A thin navy bracket-like line frames the callout text at its top left and bottom, overlapping the photo's left edge. A faint light-blue horizontal line fades in at the left edge under the subtitle. A thin white strip forms the footer.

**Design**

Background is a dark navy at top (#12285A) fading to mid blue (#2F74C0) and then to pale blue-grey near the bottom. Title, chart labels, subtitle and callout are white (#FFFFFF). The '<5%' is bold navy (#112C57). The typeface is BMW Group Condensed (a narrow sans-serif), regular weight, with bold for '<5%'. The title is about 4.8% of slide height, the subtitle and callout about 3.3%, chart labels about 2.2%, the footnote about 1.7%. The footer is grey (#595959) at about 1.8%. The bars have thin white outlines with a soft drop shadow and square corners. The line is dark navy with small grey-blue dots (#8FB0C8).

**Visuals**

Bar chart with a line overlay. Chart title: R&D expenditure [in m€, HGB] I R&D ratio [in %]. Six columns on an x-axis baseline in light grey: 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025 and YTD 09-2026. Bars are outlined in white with a translucent blue gradient fill; values in m€: 2021 6,870; 2022 7,178; 2023 7,755; 2024 9,078; 2025 8,319; YTD 09-2026 5,504 (this bar is separated from the others and has a gradient from light grey-blue at top to dark navy at bottom). A teal line with round markers connects the R&D ratio points above the bars: 6.2% (2021), 5.0% (2022), 5.0% (2023), 6.4% (2024), 6.2% (2025); the YTD point is a single grey-teal dot labelled 5.9%, not connected. No y-axis and no legend. On the right is a callout with a thin teal bracket line (top and left edges, plus a bottom line fading right) enclosing the text 'Strategic target of 4-5% to be achieved no later than 2027.'. At far right is a tall boxed, cropped photo/render of a dark teal-black battery pack, 'THE HEART' logo embossed on its top right, with glowing green-teal light edges, set against a dark background of glowing green circuit-trace lines. A short fading light-blue horizontal line accent sits at the left edge under the chart subtitle.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy at top to bright blue and then pale light blue at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title and chart text are white; accent teal #269CA6 for the line, markers and the highlighted 4-5%; footer text grey #595959. Typeface is BMW Group Condensed (a condensed sans-serif), regular weight, with bold for 4-5%. Title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height; subtitle and callout text about 3.3%; chart labels about 2.2%; footnote about 1.7%. Bars have thin white outlines, no rounded corners, and a slight shadow.

**Layout**

Two-line title across the top left. Chart subtitle below it at left, bar chart occupying the left ~55% of the slide, a tall photo panel on the right edge, and a bracketed callout with two expectation figures overlapping the photo's left edge. Footnote right-aligned above a white footer strip with the source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Bar chart of free cash flow, automotive segment, in m€, with white-outlined blue bars on a baseline and no y-axis. Categories and values: 2021 6,354; 2022 6,071 solid plus a hatched (white diagonal stripes on blue) segment on top labelled ~5,000 BBA Full Consolidation Effect, with total 11,071 above; 2023 6,942; 2024 4,852; 2025 3,240; YTD 09-2026 724 (small dark-blue bar, separated at right with its own short baseline). No legend. Right: cropped photograph of a dark navy BMW car bonnet with the BMW roundel, lit with soft highlights, full-height to the right edge, with a blue tint blending into the slide background. A thin beige/gold bracket line (top and left, plus bottom and right segments) frames the Expectation 2026 and Strategic target text. A short pale-blue gradient accent line sits at the left edge under the subtitle.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from dark navy #10264F at top to bright blue #3A7CC6 and light blue #8DB5DD near the bottom, with a white footer strip. Deep navy to brighter blue vertical gradient background (#10264F at top to #2F72BE mid to #7FAFDD at bottom) with a white footer strip. Title white (#FFFFFF), all caps, BMW Group Condensed regular (narrow sans-serif), about 4.8% of slide height. Body and chart labels white, same condensed sans at 2.2-3.3%. Callout figures in beige (#D8BDA2) bold condensed at 4.8%. Footer text grey (#595959) at 1.8%. Bars have thin white outlines with slight drop shadow; hatched segment uses white diagonal stripes.

**Layout**

Full-width title in two lines at top left over a dark navy band. Left column holds a full-height car photo from y 20% to 92%. Right of it, a chart subtitle and a callout text box with a bracket line sit in the left-middle, and a bar chart with a line series fills the right 45%. A footnote block runs across the bottom above a white footer strip with the source at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left (about 27% wide, 71% tall, starting below the title band): a dark grey MINI Cooper hatchback seen from behind driving fast through a curved tunnel of lattice ribs, with a red barrier at the left and a New York licence plate reading TNI-89X2. Motion blur on the road and walls, warm beige and teal sky through the ribs, cropped at the left edge of the slide. A short white horizontal line with a fading gradient sits at the top of the callout text, and a thin beige bracket outline (top and right edges) frames the 'We continue' callout. Chart: combo chart on a blue gradient. Eleven vertical bars outlined in white with a dark blue translucent fill, one per year 2015 to 2025, showing total dividend payout in m€: 2015 2,102; 2016 2,300; 2017 2,630; 2018 2,303; 2019 1,646; 2020 1,253; 2021 3,827; 2022 5,430 (footnote 3); 2023 3,781; 2024 2,649; 2025 2,654. The 2025 bar has a gradient fill from white-blue at the top to dark navy at the bottom. A beige line above the bars shows the payout ratio: 32.9%, 33.3%, 30.2%, 32.0%, 32.8%, 32.5%, 30.7%, 30.3%, 33.5%, 36.3%, 36.4%. It dips at 2017, flattens around 2021 to 2022 and rises sharply to 2024 and 2025. A white baseline runs under the bars with year labels below. Under it is a table row 'Dividend per ordinary share [in €]' with thin white lines above and below the values: 3,20 3,50 4,00 3,50 2,50 1,90 5,80 8,50 6,00 4,30 4,40. No legend, no axis scale. No logos.

Background: Vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2750 at the top to mid blue #3A80C8 at the bottom, with a white footer strip at the bottom 4%. Deep navy #0F2750 header band fading into a vertical gradient that turns lighter blue (#3A80C8) at the bottom. White text in the BMW Group Technical Condensed typeface (sans-serif condensed), regular weight. Title is uppercase, about 4.8% of slide height. Chart subtitle and callout about 3.3%. stock exchange for around € 4.8 bn.

**Layout**

Dark navy title band across the top with a two-line left-aligned title. Below, a photo occupies the left quarter, while the right area holds a guidance statement, three stacked translucent text boxes in the middle column and a stacked bar chart in the right column. Footnotes run beneath the content; a white footer strip holds a left-aligned source line and a right-aligned page number.

**Visuals**

Left: photograph of the BMW Headquarters (the 'Four Cylinder' tower with the BMW logo on top) beside a low grey industrial building with a street and green-white pedestrian crossing in the foreground, in daylight with a blue sky, cropped to a box on the left, running from the title band down to the footer area. A thin white horizontal line with a fading right end sits at the photo's right edge above the text boxes. Three semi-transparent steel-blue rounded-less rectangles hold the text. Right: stacked bar chart 'Total Payout' with y axis 0.0 to 8.0 in bn. € (gridlines every 2.0, white thin lines). Four bars, each with a lower teal segment (Dividends to BMW AG Shareholders) and an upper light grey-blue segment (Share Buyback): 2022 5.4 + 1.3 = 6.7; 2023 3.8 + 1.2 = 5.0; 2024 2.6 + 1.0 = 3,6; 2025 2.7 (footnote 3) + 1.3 = 4,0. Totals are printed above the bars. Legend below the chart with small square swatches: Share Buyback (light grey-blue), Dividends to BMW AG Shareholders (teal) with superscript 2.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy at the top to bright mid blue in the lower area, fading to light blue at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title and body text are white, in the BMW Group Condensed typeface (a narrow sans-serif, regular weight). Title is uppercase at about 4.8% of slide height, guidance text about 3.3%, box text about 2.6%. The first lines of each box are dark navy #133155 and the remaining lines are white. The boxes are #82A6C1 at 70% opacity with square corners and no shadows. Chart bars are teal #548D9E and light blue-grey #85ACB9. Footer text is grey #595959. The words 'Our strategic guidance' are underlined.

### Slide 57

**Text**

OUTLOOK 2026.
BMW Group
Segment Automotive
Segment Motorcycle
Segment Financial
Services
Profit before tax:
Significant decrease.
Size of workforce:
Slight decrease.
Deliveries:
Moderate decrease.
EBIT margin:
-0.5 to 0.5%.
ROCE:
-1 to 1%.
Deliveries:
Slight decrease.
EBIT margin:
4 to 6%.
ROCE:
10 to 14%.
Return on equity:
13 to16%.
Unless specific ranges are specified, the BMW Group uses the terminology and ranges, to be found in the BMW Group Report Glossary, as a basis when forecasting key performance indicators.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 57 -

**Layout**

Title top-left on a dark blue field. Four equal columns (cards) in a row from about y 20% to 94%, each with a photo on top and a blue text panel below, outlined in thin white lines. Disclaimer line centered below the cards, white footer strip with left-aligned source text and right-aligned page number.

**Visuals**

Four photos at the top of the cards, each with a dark-blue-to-transparent fade at the top behind the heading. 1) BMW Group: the BMW Headquarters four-cylinder tower and the silver bowl-shaped BMW Museum, a green lawn in front, bright pale blue sky. 2) Segment Automotive: a dark grey BMW sedan seen from the front and a green BMW wagon seen from the rear, parked on a rooftop with city buildings behind and a warm hazy sun glow. 3) Segment Motorcycle: two adventure motorcycles, one blue-white and one dark, on a rocky hillside with green shrubs, mountains and a low sun flaring on the horizon. 4) Segment Financial Services: a smiling grey-haired woman with glasses and a blue blazer holds papers across a round wooden table from a curly red-haired woman seen from behind, in a bright showroom with cars behind. Thin white borders surround each card. Thin horizontal gold-beige line accents sit at the far left and right edges at about y 68%. Each card has a thin white line under the photo.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2A5C at top to bright blue #3A80C8 at bottom, with a white footer strip at the bottom. Vertical gradient background from deep navy #0F2A5C at top to bright blue #3A80C8 near the bottom. The title and all card text are white in the BMW Group Technical Narrow Condensed sans-serif. Labels are bold and values regular, at about 3.3% of slide height. The title is about 4.8% of slide height, upper case, regular weight. Thin white 2px card outlines, no rounded corners, no shadows. The card text panels are a slightly translucent blue. The footer is a white strip with grey #595959 text.

### Slide 58

**Text**

ALREADY AHEAD.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 58 -

**Layout**

Split composition: the left half is a full-height photograph of a car silhouette at sunrise that fades into a blue gradient on the right. A thin white open-corner frame sits right of centre, with the title left-aligned inside it. A footer at the bottom left and a page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left half: a photograph of a dark, nearly black silhouetted car seen head-on in a desert at sunrise, with a bright sun glow behind the roof and dark mountains on the horizon. The sky goes from steel blue-grey at the top to warm gold and orange near the horizon. The foreground is almost black. The car's twin kidney grille and slim headlight signature are outlined in glowing white. The photo's right edge fades smoothly into the blue background. Right half: a vertical gradient from deep navy at the top to bright medium blue at the bottom. A thin white frame outlines the title: a line along the top from the left corner, fading out toward the right, a vertical line down the left side that fades at the bottom, and a line along the bottom fading in from the left, with a right vertical line at the right edge. No logos.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2A5C at the top to bright blue #3A80C8 at the bottom, with a sunrise car photograph on the left half fading into it. Dark-to-bright blue gradient background (navy #0F2A5C at the top to #3A80C8 at the bottom) with a photograph on the left. The title is white (#FFFFFF), uppercase, in a condensed sans-serif (BMW Group TN Condensed, regular), about 4.8% of slide height. Footer text is grey #595959, condensed sans-serif, about 1.8% of slide height. The frame is a thin white line of about 2px with fading ends, no shadow, no rounded corners.

**Layout**

Two-line white title top-left over a dark blue background. Below it a grid of 10 portrait cards in 2 rows of 5, each card with a photo, a white diagonal wedge across the lower left carrying name, role and a phone line. Footnote right-aligned under the grid; white footer strip with left footer text and right page number.

**Visuals**

Ten studio portrait photos of the IR team in business attire, each against the same blurred glass-walled office background with greenery and some red accents. Top row: Adam Sykes, a dark-haired man in a blue suit and white open-collar shirt, smiling; Holger Fuetterer, a man with short grey-brown hair in a dark jacket with white pocket square, arms crossed; Martina Hilmer, a woman with long wavy brown hair and glasses, pink knit top, dark blazer; Julia Krause, a woman with long dark hair, necklace, dark blazer over white top; Patrick Neumayer, a man with short brown hair in a navy blazer and white open shirt. Bottom row: Nicolai Pest, a bearded man with styled dark hair, dark suit and white shirt; Tobias Pfanzelt, a dark-haired man with light stubble in a navy jacket and white shirt; Sabine Rabus, a woman with dark curly hair, navy blazer over light top; Meike Sauvagerd, a woman with long blonde hair, dark blazer over pink top; Andreas Stoeffler, a man with short brown hair, dark jacket over light blue shirt, arms crossed, bluer background. Each card has a diagonal triangular overlay from the left edge rising towards the lower right, white for nine cards and dark navy #173B68 for the first card. Each phone line has a small outlined envelope icon with motion lines in front of the number, in a thin white strip under the wedge (navy for the first card). The cards have a thin white border. The email address in the title is underlined.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2A55 at the top to mid blue #2F73B8 near the bottom, ending above a white footer strip. Title in white all-caps BMW Group Technical Narrow Condensed regular, about 4.8% of slide height, with bold I separators. Card names in navy #133155, first name regular and surname bold at about 3% of height; roles and phones regular at about 2.2%. The first card text is white. Footnote white at about 1.7%. Footer grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Flat design, no shadows or rounded corners, thin white card outlines.

**Layout**

Dark-blue slide with title at top left. A tall photo is boxed at the left from about 21% to 92% of the height. The subtitle sits to the right of the photo, and a horizontal timeline arrow with month labels and quarter dividers runs across the lower-middle. Three event callouts hang above/below the timeline. A white footer strip at the bottom holds the source text on the left and the page number on the right.

**Visuals**

Photograph of the BMW Headquarters (the Four Cylinder tower) and the BMW Museum bowl, with a curved grey pedestrian ramp and green lawn in the foreground. Shot from a low angle against a blue sky with soft white clouds in daylight. Boxed at the left edge, cropped to a portrait rectangle. A short white horizontal accent line fading to transparent on the right sits at the photo's right edge, under the subtitle. The timeline is a tan/beige horizontal arrow pointing right, with white vertical quarter dividers at Jan start, Apr, Jul, Oct and the end after Dec. Small white tick marks separate the months. Thin tan vertical leader lines connect the events to the timeline: 11/03/27 (Mar) and 12/05/27 (May) go upward, and 05/05/27, 29/07/27 and 04/11/27 go downward. The BMW roundel logo sits on top of the tower in the photo.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #10264F at the top to mid blue #2C74C4 and then pale blue-grey #8FA9C8 at the bottom, above a white footer strip. White text throughout in BMW Group Condensed (a narrow sans-serif). The title is regular weight in capitals at about 4.8% of the slide height. The subtitle and dates are regular, while event names and quarter labels are bold. The footer is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Tan lines #D9B99B are used for the timeline and leader lines. There are no shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 61

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 7 and 10 add a roadmap triangle and brand grid, and slides 61 to 65 are plain disclaimer and data tables.

**Text**

PLEASE FIND MORE INFORMATION TO THE FOLLOWING TOPICS VIA THE LINKS.
BMW Group Company Reports.
Telephone Conferences.
ESG Overview.
Corporate Events.
Annual General Meeting.
Share Buy-Back.
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 61 -

**Layout**

Full-width title at top left. A photo of the BMW Tower is placed bleeding off the left edge in the lower half. A 3x2 grid of translucent tiles (3 columns, 2 rows) sits on the right with centred underlined link text. A white footer strip at the bottom holds the footer text on the left and the page number on the right.

**Visuals**

Left photograph (about 0–26.6% wide, 20.6–92% tall, cropped at the left slide edge): the BMW Four Cylinder tower with the BMW roundel on top, the bowl-shaped BMW Museum to its right, a curved white walkway/ramp with a railing in the foreground curving from lower left, green lawn at lower right, autumn trees at the left, bright blue sky with soft white clouds. Daylight, cool blue and white tones with green grass. Over the photo's right edge is a thin white horizontal line that fades to transparent toward the right (about 24–35% x, 56% y). Six semi-transparent white tiles (50% opacity), square-cornered, no outline, no shadow, each with underlined white hyperlink text. Tiles are slightly greyer at the top and lighter blue at the bottom because of the gradient behind them.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from dark navy (#10264F) at the top through royal blue (#2F78C4) to pale blue (#8FB0CC) near the bottom, above a white footer band. Title is white, uppercase, condensed sans-serif (BMW Group Condensed style), about 4.8% of slide height. Link text is white, underlined, condensed sans-serif, about 3.3% of height. Footer text is grey (#595959) condensed sans-serif, about 1.8% of height. Tiles are white at 50% opacity with sharp corners.

### Slide 62

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 7 and 10 add a roadmap triangle and brand grid, and slides 61 to 65 are plain disclaimer and data tables.

**Text**

DISCLAIMER.
This document contains forward-looking statements that reflect BMW Group’s current views about future events. The words “anticipate,” “assume,” “believe,” “estimate,” “expect,” “intend,” “may,” ”can,” “could,” “plan,” “project,” “should” and similar expressions are used to identify forward-looking statements.
These statements are subject to many risks and uncertainties or may be affected by factors outside BMW Group’s control, including adverse developments in global economic conditions resulting in a decline in demand in BMW Group’s key markets, including China, North America and Europe; a deterioration in credit and financial markets; a shift in consumer preferences affecting demand for BMW Group’s products; changes in the prices of fuel or raw materials; disruption of production due to shortages of materials, labor strikes or supplier insolvencies; the effective implementation of BMW Group’s strategic goals and targets; changes in laws, regulations and government policies, particularly those relating to vehicle emissions, fuel economy and safety; and other risks and uncertainties, including those described under the heading “Report on Risks and Opportunities” in BMW Group’s most recent Annual Report.
If any of these risks and uncertainties materializes or if the assumptions underlying any of BMW Group’s forward-looking statements prove to be incorrect, actual results may be materially different from those BMW Group expresses or implies by such statements. BMW Group does not intend or assume any obligation to update these forward-looking statements.
For consumption and emissions data, please visit our homepage: www.bmw.com
BMW Group Investor Relations I November 2026.
- 62 -

**Layout**

Title at top left on a dark blue band. A tall photo is boxed at the left edge, below the title, flush to the left. Four paragraphs of left-aligned body text fill the right side from x about 28% to 96%. A white footer strip at the bottom has the source line at left and the page number at right.

**Visuals**

One photograph at the left, cropped as a vertical rectangle, flush with the left edge. It shows the BMW Headquarters four-cylinder tower in Munich with the BMW Museum bowl at right. The tower is cream and silver with a BMW logo on top. In the foreground a pale curved walkway with a railing leads in from the lower left, beside a green lawn and autumn trees. The sky is pale blue with soft white clouds, in bright daylight. The slide background is a dark navy to light blue vertical gradient. The body text is underlined only on the link www.bmw.com, which is bold. There are no other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #11284F at the top to light blue #3A7BC8 and #8AA6C8 at the bottom, with a white footer strip at the bottom edge. The background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #11284F at the top to mid blue #3A7BC8 and then light blue-grey #8AA6C8 near the bottom, above a white footer strip. Title and body text are white #FFFFFF. Footer text is grey #595959. The typeface is BMW Group TN Condensed, a condensed sans-serif. Body is regular weight, with the link in bold and underlined. The title is about 4.8% of slide height and the body about 3% of slide height, with generous line spacing and extra gaps between paragraphs. There are no shadows or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Title at top left with 4% left margin. Below it a full-width two-column table (narrow model column, wide consumption data column) with a header row and 11 data rows, bounded by thin white rules at top and bottom. A white footer strip at the bottom holds the source line on the left and the page number on the right.

**Visuals**

Table with columns 'Modell' and 'Consumption Data' and 11 rows: BMW i3 xDrive50; BMW iX3 xDrive50; BMW iX4 M60 xDrive; BMW iX xDrive60; Mini Aceman JCW; Rolls-Royce Spectre; Mini Cooper S Cabrio; BMW i7 xDrive60; BMW R 12 S; BMW X1 xDrive23i; BMW i5 eDrive 40 Touring; BMW iX2 xDrive30. Each row lists its consumption and CO2 values as in the text above. Alternate rows (1, 3, 5, 7, 9, 11) have a translucent light grey-blue band across both columns. Thin white horizontal lines sit above the header, below the header and below the last row. CO2 uses subscript 2. No images or logos.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from navy #0F2850 at the top to bright blue #2B6CB8 and light blue #7FAAD6 at the bottom, with a white footer strip across the bottom 4%. Deep navy #0F2850 at top blending to bright blue #2B6CB8 mid-slide and light blue #7FAAD6 at bottom, in a vertical gradient. White text, BMW Group Condensed typeface (narrow sans-serif). Title is regular weight uppercase, about 4.8% of slide height. Header is bold at about 3.7%. Body is regular at about 3% . Row bands are #DEE5EC at 20% opacity with square corners, no shadows. Footer strip is white with grey #595959 text at about 1.8%.

**Layout**

Title at top left. Below it, a full-width two-column table (model name column about 15% wide, consumption data column about 77% wide) with a header row and ten data rows, starting at the left margin of 4% and ending at about 96%. White footer strip at the bottom with the source line at left and page number at right.

**Visuals**

A two-column table with a thin white rule above the header and another below the last row. Header: 'Modell' and 'Consumption Data'. Ten data rows with the model names and consumption texts listed in the slide text. Odd-numbered rows (1, 3, 5, 7, 9 and the last row) have a translucent light grey-blue band (#DEE5EC at 20% opacity). Even rows are unfilled so the gradient shows through. The last row (BMW M760e xDrive Limousine) is two lines tall. CO2 is written with a subscript 2. No images, charts or logos beyond the background.

**Design**

Background is a vertical gradient from deep navy #0F2850 at the top through royal blue #2A66B8 in the lower middle to pale steel blue #8AA8C8 at the bottom of the 95.8% tall image. A white footer strip fills the bottom. Title and table text are white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Condensed (a condensed sans-serif). The title is regular weight in capitals at about 4.8% of slide height. Table headers are bold at about 3.7%, body text regular at about 3% with subscripts at about 2%. The footer text is grey #595959 at about 1.8%. Thin white rules, no shadows, no rounded corners.

### Slide 65

**Review notes**

- Review: Slides 7 and 10 add a roadmap triangle and brand grid, and slides 61 to 65 are plain disclaimer and data tables.

**Text**

**Layout**

White-footer slide with a left-aligned all-caps title at top left. Below it a two-column table spanning about 4% to 96% of the width, with a header row and three data rows. Footer strip at the bottom with the source line at left and the page number at right, both on the 4% margins.

**Visuals**

Full-width blue gradient background image covering the top 95.8% of the slide, dark navy at top fading to mid blue and then to pale grey-blue at the bottom edge. A table with thin light-blue horizontal rules above the header, below the header and below the last row. Data rows 1 and 3 and the left cells have a translucent pale grey-blue fill (#DEE5EC at 20% opacity), and row 2 is similarly filled. The table has 2 columns: Modell and Consumption Data, with 3 rows: BMW X5 xDrive40i, BMW XM, BMW X5 M60e x Drive. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Vertical gradient from dark navy #0F2850 at top through blue #2A66B5 to pale grey-blue #8DA9C6, with a white strip at the bottom. Dark navy #0F2850 at top fading into royal blue #2A66B5 mid-slide and light grey-blue #8DA9C6 at the bottom, with a white footer strip. Title white #FFFFFF in BMW Group Technical Narrow Condensed Regular, all caps, about 4.8% of slide height. Table headers white bold condensed sans-serif about 3.7% height. Body text white condensed sans-serif regular about 3% height. Footer text grey #595959 condensed sans-serif about 1.8% height. Table rules are thin light blue (#B8D0F0). No shadows or rounded corners.
